<script>
// Global program name variable for dataLayer tracking
let globalProgramName = '';

// Global video URL variable - set based on acadPlanCode
let videoUrl = '';

// Initialize global variables when DOM is loaded
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const programNameElement = document.getElementById('program-name');
    globalProgramName = programNameElement ? programNameElement.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
    
    // Set video URL from JSP variable
    videoUrl = 'https://player.vimeo.com/video/936819161';
});

// Video functionality - dynamic based on videoUrl
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Ensure videoUrl is set (may not be if the other DOMContentLoaded hasn't fired yet)
    if (!videoUrl) videoUrl = 'https://player.vimeo.com/video/936819161';
    if (!videoUrl) return;
    
    const playBtn = document.getElementById('play-video-btn');
    const watchLink = document.getElementById('watch-video-link');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-video-btn');
    const overlay = document.getElementById('video-overlay');
    const player = document.getElementById('video-player');
    const iframe = document.getElementById('youtube-iframe');
    
    function playVideo(clickedElement = 'play button') {
        // Hide overlay
        if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
        // Show video player
        if (player) player.style.display = 'block';
        // Update iframe src to autoplay using dynamic videoUrl.
        // Use & if the URL already carries query params, otherwise ?
        if (iframe) iframe.src = videoUrl + (videoUrl.indexOf('?') > -1 ? '&' : '?') + 'autoplay=1';
        
        // Track video play event with dynamic text based on clicked element
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "get to know the accountancy degree at asu",
                "text": clickedElement
            });
        }
    }
    
    function closeVideo() {
        // Hide video player
        if (player) player.style.display = 'none';
        // Show overlay
        if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'block';
        // Stop video by clearing the iframe src, then resetting it without autoplay.
        // Clearing first ensures the browser fully unloads the video player.
        if (iframe) {
            iframe.src = '';
            iframe.src = videoUrl;
        }

        // Track video close. Section matches the open event so the pair reports together.
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "close",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "get to know the accountancy degree at asu",
                "text": "close cross"
            });
        }
    }
    
    // Add click handlers
    if (playBtn) {
        playBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            playVideo('play button');
        });
    }
    if (watchLink) {
        watchLink.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            playVideo('watch video');
        });
    }
    if (closeBtn) {
        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeVideo);
    }

    // YouTube posters are set server-side from a predictable thumbnail URL, but Vimeo has
    // no static thumbnail-by-ID URL, so fetch its poster from Vimeo's oEmbed API and apply
    // it as the background. Progressive enhancement: if the lookup fails, the solid
    // background color remains (no regression).
    var vimeoMatch = videoUrl.match(/player\.vimeo\.com\/video\/(\d+)/);
    if (vimeoMatch) {
        var vimeoId = vimeoMatch[1];
        var background = document.getElementById('video-background');
        if (background) {
            fetch('https://vimeo.com/api/oembed.json?width=1280&url=' + encodeURIComponent('https://vimeo.com/' + vimeoId))
                .then(function (response) { return response.ok ? response.json() : null; })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data && data.thumbnail_url) {
                        background.style.backgroundImage =
                            "linear-gradient(rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)), url('" + data.thumbnail_url + "')";
                        background.style.backgroundPosition = 'top center';
                    }
                })
                .catch(function () { /* Keep the solid background if the lookup fails */ });
        }
    }
});

// Tab navigation functionality (anchor-based)
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {

// Tab navigation functionality (anchor-based)
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#mobile-nav-menu a');
    
    // Update active tab based on scroll position or hash
    function updateActiveTab() {
        const sections = document.querySelectorAll('[id^="quick-facts"], [id^="overview"], [id^="courses"], [id^="careers"], [id^="admissions"], [id^="cost"], [id^="contact-info"]');
        let currentSection = '';
        
        // Check if there's a hash in URL
        if (window.location.hash) {
            currentSection = window.location.hash.substring(1);
        } else {
            // Find which section is currently in view
            sections.forEach(section => {
                const rect = section.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (rect.top <= 100 && rect.bottom >= 100) {
                    currentSection = section.id;
                }
            });
        }
        
        // Update active tab
        tabs.forEach(tab => {
            tab.classList.remove('active');
            if (tab.getAttribute('href') === '#' + currentSection) {
                tab.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
        
        // If no section is active, make first tab active
        if (!currentSection && tabs.length > 0) {
            tabs[0].classList.add('active');
        }
    }
    
    // Smooth scroll to section when tab is clicked
    tabs.forEach(tab => {
        tab.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            const href = this.getAttribute('href');
            if (!href || href.charAt(0) !== '#') {
                return;
            }
            e.preventDefault();
            const targetId = href.substring(1);
            const targetSection = document.getElementById(targetId);
            
            if (targetSection) {
                // Update URL hash
                history.pushState(null, null, '#' + targetId);
                
                // Smooth scroll to section with manual offset
                const offset = 120; // px below viewport top
                const elementPosition = targetSection.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset;
                const scrollTarget = elementPosition - offset;
                window.scrollTo({
                    top: scrollTarget,
                    behavior: 'smooth'
                });
                
                // Update active tab
                updateActiveTab();
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Update active tab on scroll
    window.addEventListener('scroll', updateActiveTab);
    
    // Update active tab on page load
    updateActiveTab();
    
    // Load general studies descriptions for static content
    // REMOVED: No longer needed
    
    // Course accordion functionality
    const courseHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.course-year-header');
    courseHeaders.forEach(header => {
        header.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const content = this.nextElementSibling;
            const arrow = this.querySelector('.course-year-arrow');
            const isOpen = content.classList.contains('open');
            
            // Get year information for dataLayer tracking
            const yearSpan = this.querySelector('span:not(.course-year-arrow)');
            let yearText = '';
            let sectionName = 'sample courses'; // default for undergraduate
            
            if (yearSpan) {
                yearText = yearSpan.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
            }
            
            // Check if this is a graduate category
            if (this.closest('.graduate-category')) {
                sectionName = 'degree details';
            }
            
            // Toggle current accordion only (don't close others)
            if (isOpen) {
                // Close this year - keep normal speed
                content.style.transition = 'max-height 0.8s ease-out, opacity 0.6s ease-out, transform 0.6s ease-out';
                content.classList.remove('open');
                content.setAttribute('inert', '');
                this.classList.remove('open');
                this.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                if (arrow) {
                    arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)'; // Point down when closed
                }
                
                // DataLayer tracking for close
                if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined' && yearText) {
                    dataLayer.push({
                        "event": "collapse",
                        "action": "close",
                        "name": "onclick",
                        "type": "click",
                        "region": "main content",
                        "section": sectionName,
                        "text": yearText
                    });
                }
            } else {
                // Open this year - make it slower
                content.style.transition = 'max-height 1.5s ease-out, opacity 1.2s ease-out, transform 1.2s ease-out';
                content.classList.add('open');
                content.removeAttribute('inert');
                this.classList.add('open');
                this.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                if (arrow) {
                    arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)'; // Point up when open
                }
                
                // DataLayer tracking for open
                if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined' && yearText) {
                    dataLayer.push({
                        "event": "collapse",
                        "action": "open",
                        "name": "onclick",
                        "type": "click",
                        "region": "main content",
                        "section": sectionName,
                        "text": yearText
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Open first year by default (desktop only)
    if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
        const firstYearHeader = document.querySelector('.course-year-header');
        if (firstYearHeader && firstYearHeader.querySelector('.course-year-number').textContent === '1') {
            firstYearHeader.classList.add('open');
            firstYearHeader.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
            const firstYearContent = firstYearHeader.nextElementSibling;
            const firstYearArrow = firstYearHeader.querySelector('.course-year-arrow');
            if (firstYearContent) {
                firstYearContent.classList.add('open');
                firstYearContent.removeAttribute('inert');
            }
            if (firstYearArrow) {
                firstYearArrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)'; // Point up when open
            }
        }
    } else {
        // On mobile, close all years by default
        document.querySelectorAll('.course-year-content').forEach(c => {
            c.classList.remove('open');
            // For graduate categories, also remove inline style to ensure they close
            if (c.parentElement.classList.contains('graduate-category')) {
                c.style.display = '';
            }
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('.course-year-header').forEach(h => {
            h.classList.remove('open');
            const arrow = h.querySelector('.course-year-arrow');
            if (arrow) {
                // Use consistent rotation for all categories (both undergraduate and graduate)
                arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
            }
        });
    }
});

// Initialize graduate categories to be open on desktop
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.graduate-category .course-year-content').forEach(c => {
            c.classList.add('open');
            c.removeAttribute('inert');
            c.style.display = 'block';
            
            // Make course items visible by adding animate class
            const courseItems = c.querySelectorAll('.course-item');
            courseItems.forEach(item => {
                item.classList.add('animate');
            });
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('.graduate-category .course-year-header').forEach(h => {
            h.classList.add('open');
            h.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
            const arrow = h.querySelector('.course-year-arrow');
            if (arrow) {
                // Use consistent rotation for open graduate categories
                arrow.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
            }
        });
    }
});

// Dynamic toggle function for overview show more/less
function toggleOverviewDynamic(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const contentContainer = document.getElementById('overview-content');
    const toggleLink = document.getElementById('overview-toggle');
    
    if (!contentContainer || !toggleLink) {
        console.error('Overview elements not found');
        return;
    }
    
    console.log('Toggle clicked, current state:', {
        isCollapsed: contentContainer.classList.contains('collapsed'),
        currentHeight: contentContainer.style.maxHeight,
        scrollHeight: contentContainer.scrollHeight,
        buttonText: toggleLink.textContent.trim()
    });
    
    // Get the dynamic section name from the overview heading
    const overviewHeadingEl = document.getElementById('overview-heading');
    const sectionName = overviewHeadingEl ? overviewHeadingEl.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() : 'degree description';
    
    const currentText = toggleLink.textContent.trim();
    const isExpanding = currentText === 'Read more';
    
    if (isExpanding) {
        // Expanding: set max-height to scrollHeight
        contentContainer.style.maxHeight = contentContainer.scrollHeight + 'px';
        contentContainer.classList.remove('collapsed');
        toggleLink.textContent = 'Show less';
        toggleLink.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        console.log('Expanding to:', contentContainer.scrollHeight + 'px');
        
        // DataLayer tracking for expand
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push({
                'event': 'collapse',
                'action': 'open',
                'name': 'onclick',
                'type': 'click',
                'region': 'main content',
                'section': sectionName,
                'text': 'read more'
            });
        }
    } else {
        // Collapsing: set to scrollHeight first, then to collapsed height
        contentContainer.style.maxHeight = contentContainer.scrollHeight + 'px';
        setTimeout(() => {
            contentContainer.classList.add('collapsed');
            contentContainer.style.maxHeight = '';
        }, 10);
        toggleLink.textContent = 'Read more';
        toggleLink.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        console.log('Collapsing to 200px');
        
        // DataLayer tracking for collapse
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push({
                'event': 'collapse',
                'action': 'close',
                'name': 'onclick',
                'type': 'click',
                'region': 'main content',
                'section': sectionName,
                'text': 'show less'
            });
        }
    }
}

// Toggle course details expansion
function toggleCourseDetails(button, year, courseIndex) {
    const detailsDiv = document.getElementById('course-details-' + year + '-' + courseIndex);
    const isExpanded = detailsDiv.classList.contains('open');
    const svg = button.querySelector('svg path');
    
    // Get course title for dataLayer tracking - get all span text from course-details
    const courseItem = button.closest('.course-item');
    let courseTitle = '';
    let sectionName = '';
    
    if (courseItem) {
        // Get all text from span elements in course-details, excluding spans with class "course-credits" and "desktop-divider"
        const courseDetails = courseItem.querySelector('.course-details');
        const spanElements = courseDetails.querySelectorAll('span:not(.course-credits):not(.desktop-divider)');
        const spanTexts = Array.from(spanElements).map(span => span.textContent.trim()).filter(text => text);
        courseTitle = spanTexts.join(' ').toLowerCase();
        
        // Determine section name based on year parameter
        if (year.startsWith('cat')) {
            // Graduate category
            const categoryHeader = courseItem.closest('.graduate-category');
            if (categoryHeader) {
                const categoryTitle = categoryHeader.querySelector('.course-year-header span:not(.course-year-arrow)');
                if (categoryTitle) {
                    sectionName = categoryTitle.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
                }
            }
        } else {
            // Undergraduate year
            if (year === '1') {
                sectionName = 'first year';
            } else if (year === '2') {
                sectionName = 'second year';
            } else if (year === '3') {
                sectionName = 'third year';
            } else if (year === '4') {
                sectionName = 'fourth year';
            } else {
                sectionName = 'year ' + year;
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (isExpanded) {
        detailsDiv.classList.remove('open');
        detailsDiv.setAttribute('inert', '');
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        // Change to plus icon
        svg.setAttribute('d', 'M12 5V19M5 12H19');
        
        // DataLayer tracking for collapse
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined' && courseTitle && sectionName) {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "collapse",
                "action": "close",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": sectionName,
                "text": courseTitle
            });
        }
    } else {
        detailsDiv.classList.add('open');
        detailsDiv.removeAttribute('inert');
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        // Change to minus icon
        svg.setAttribute('d', 'M5 12H19');
        
        // DataLayer tracking for expand
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined' && courseTitle && sectionName) {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "collapse",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": sectionName,
                "text": courseTitle
            });
        }
        
        // Load course descriptions only when expanding and if not already loaded
        const loadingDiv = detailsDiv.querySelector('.description-loading');
        if (loadingDiv && !detailsDiv.hasAttribute('data-loaded')) {
            // Mark as loaded to prevent multiple API calls
            detailsDiv.setAttribute('data-loaded', 'true');
            
            // Get course data from data attributes
            const courseData = JSON.parse(detailsDiv.getAttribute('data-courses') || '[]');
            const minGrade = detailsDiv.getAttribute('data-min-grade') || '';
            const generalStudiesCode = detailsDiv.getAttribute('data-general-studies') || '';
            
            if (courseData.length > 0) {
                loadCourseDescriptions('course-details-' + year + '-' + courseIndex, courseData, minGrade);
            } else if (generalStudiesCode) {
                // Display simple hardcoded message for general studies
                const loadingDiv = detailsDiv.querySelector('.description-loading');
                if (loadingDiv) {
                    loadingDiv.innerHTML = '<div><strong>Description:</strong> This is a general studies requirement.</div>';
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Fix Roman numerals in course titles that may have been incorrectly title-cased
function fixRomanNumerals(title) {
    if (!title) return title;
    
    // Common Roman numerals pattern - matches word boundaries before Roman numerals
    const romanNumeralPattern = /\b([Ii]{1,3}|[Ii]v|V[Ii]{0,3}|[Ii]x|X[Ii]{0,3}|X[Ll]|[Ll][Xx]{0,3})\b/g;
    
    return title.replace(romanNumeralPattern, function(match) {
        // Convert to uppercase to fix incorrectly cased Roman numerals
        return match.toUpperCase();
    });
}

// Load course descriptions from JSON API
function loadCourseDescriptions(containerId, courses, minGrade) {
    const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
    const loadingDiv = container.querySelector('.description-loading');
    
    if (courses.length === 0) {
        loadingDiv.innerHTML = '<div><strong>Description:</strong><br>No specific courses defined for this requirement.</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    // Array to store course descriptions in original order
    let courseDescriptions = new Array(courses.length);
    let courseTitles = new Array(courses.length); // Store course titles for connector display
    let generalStudiesInfo = [];
    let loadedCount = 0;
    
    courses.forEach((course, index) => {
		console.log("course connector:::::", course.connector);
        // Use the new JSON API endpoint
        const apiUrl = '/tooltipdynamic/course2json/' + course.subject + '_' + course.catalogNumber + '_';
        
        fetch(apiUrl)
            .then(response => {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
                }
                return response.json(); // Parse as JSON instead of text
            })
            .then(data => {
                let courseTitle = course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber;
                let description = 'Course description not available.';
                let generalStudies = '';
                
                // Use JSON response data
                if (data && data.success) {
                    if (data.title) {
                        courseTitle = fixRomanNumerals(data.title); // Fix Roman numerals in title
                    }
                    if (data.courseDescription) {
                        description = data.courseDescription.trim();
                    }
                    if (data.generalStudies) {
                        generalStudies = data.generalStudies.trim();
                        generalStudiesInfo.push(generalStudies);
                    }
                } else {
                    console.warn('API returned unsuccessful response for ' + course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber + ':', data);
                    if (data && data.message) {
                        description = data.message;
                    }
                }
                
                // Store course title for connector display
                courseTitles[index] = courseTitle;
                
                let courseHtml = '<div class="mt-1">';
                
                // For multiple courses, add link to each title
                if (courses.length > 1) {
                    const catalogUrl = 'https://catalog.apps.asu.edu/catalog/courses/courselist?subject=' + course.subject + '&catalogNbr=' + course.catalogNumber;
                    courseHtml += '<div class="mb-2">';
                    courseHtml += '<strong><a href="' + catalogUrl + '" target="_blank">' + courseTitle + '<span class="sr-only"> (opens in new window)</span></a></strong>';
                    courseHtml += ': ' + description;
                    courseHtml += '</div>';
                } else {
                    // For single course, show description only (general studies will be shown separately)
                    courseHtml += '<div class="mb-2">' + description + '</div>';
                }
                
                courseHtml += '</div>';
                
                // Store in the correct position to maintain order
                courseDescriptions[index] = courseHtml;
                loadedCount++;
                
                // Update the container when all descriptions are loaded
                if (loadedCount === courses.length) {
                    // Combine all course descriptions in original order
                    const descriptionsHtml = courseDescriptions.join('');
                    updateDescriptionContainer(loadingDiv, descriptionsHtml, minGrade, courses, generalStudiesInfo, courseTitles);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error loading course description for ' + course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber + ':', error);
                let courseHtml = '<div>';
                
                // Store fallback title
                courseTitles[index] = course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber;
                
                // For multiple courses, add link to each title even on error
                if (courses.length > 1) {
                    const catalogUrl = 'https://catalog.apps.asu.edu/catalog/courses/courselist?subject=' + course.subject + '&catalogNbr=' + course.catalogNumber;
                    courseHtml += '<h4 class="mb-2"><a href="' + catalogUrl + '" target="_blank">' + course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber + '<span class="sr-only"> (opens in new window)</span></a></h4>';
                } else {
                    courseHtml += '<h4 class="mb-2">' + course.subject + ' ' + course.catalogNumber + '</h4>';
                }
                
                courseHtml += '<div>Course description not available. (Error: ' + error.message + ')</div>';
                courseHtml += '</div>';
                
                // Store in the correct position to maintain order
                courseDescriptions[index] = courseHtml;
                loadedCount++;
                
                if (loadedCount === courses.length) {
                    // Combine all course descriptions in original order
                    const descriptionsHtml = courseDescriptions.join('');
                    updateDescriptionContainer(loadingDiv, descriptionsHtml, minGrade, courses, generalStudiesInfo, courseTitles);
                }
            });
    });
}

function updateDescriptionContainer(loadingDiv, descriptionsHtml, minGrade, courses, generalStudiesInfo, courseTitles) {

    // Build course titles with connectors - only display if titles are the same (except First-Year Composition)
    let courseTitlesHtml = '';
    if (courses && courses.length > 1 && courseTitles) {
        // Extract only the title part after the colon for comparison
        const titleParts = courseTitles.filter(title => title).map(title => {
            const parts = title.split(':');
            return parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : title.trim();
        });
        const uniqueTitles = [...new Set(titleParts)];
        const firstTitle = titleParts[0] || '';
        
        // Only display if all title parts are the same OR it's "First-Year Composition"
        if (uniqueTitles.length === 1 || firstTitle.toLowerCase().includes('first-year composition')) {
            courseTitlesHtml = '<div class="mb-2">';
            
            courses.forEach((course, index) => {
                if (index > 0) {
                    const connector = course.connector || 'OR';
                    
                    // For 3+ courses: AND on same line, OR on new line
                    if (courses.length >= 3) {
                        if (connector.toUpperCase() === 'AND') {
                            courseTitlesHtml += ' ' + connector + ' ';
                        } else {
                            courseTitlesHtml += '<br>' + connector + ' ';
                        }
                    } else {
                        // For 2 courses: always on same line
                        courseTitlesHtml += ' ' + connector + ' ';
                    }
                }
                // Display subject + catalog number + title (e.g., "CIS 484: Internship")
                courseTitlesHtml += courseTitles[index];
            });
            
            courseTitlesHtml += '</div>';
        }
        // If titles are different (except First-Year Composition), keep courseTitlesHtml empty
    }
    
    let finalHtml = courseTitlesHtml + '<div><strong>Description:</strong></div>' + descriptionsHtml;
    if (minGrade && minGrade !== 'null' && minGrade !== '') {
        finalHtml += '<div class="mb-2"><strong>Minimum grade:</strong> ' + minGrade + '</div>';
    }
    
    // Add general studies information after minimum grade
    if (generalStudiesInfo && generalStudiesInfo.length > 0) {
        // General studies code mapping
        const generalStudiesMapping = {
            'AMIT': 'American Institutions',
            'GCSI': 'Global Communities, Societies, and Individuals',
            'CIVI': 'Governance and Civic Engagement',
            'HUAD': 'Humanities, Arts and Design',
            'MATH': 'Mathematics',
            'QTRS': 'Quantitative Reasoning',
            'SCIT': 'Scientific Thinking in Natural Sciences',
            'SOBE': 'Social and Behavioral Sciences',
            'SUST': 'Sustainability',
            'C': 'Cultural Diversity in the U.S.',
            'CS': 'Computer/Statistics/Quantitative Applications',
            'G': 'Global Awareness',
            'H': 'Historical Awareness',
            'HU': 'Humanities, Arts and Design',
            'L': 'Literacy and Critical Inquiry',
            'MA': 'Mathematical Studies',
            'SB': 'Social-Behavioral Sciences',
            'SG': 'Natural Science - General',
            'SQ': 'Natural Science - Quantitative'
        };
        
        // Remove empty, null, or "No" values
        const validGeneralStudies = generalStudiesInfo.filter(gs => gs && gs.trim() !== '' && gs.trim().toLowerCase() !== 'no');
        
        if (validGeneralStudies.length > 0) {
            // Remove duplicates and process each general studies code
            const uniqueGeneralStudies = [...new Set(validGeneralStudies)];
            let generalStudiesList = [];
            
            uniqueGeneralStudies.forEach(gs => {
                // Split by "OR" and process each part
                const parts = gs.split(' OR ').map(part => part.trim());
                parts.forEach(part => {
                    // Check if the code exists in mapping
                    if (generalStudiesMapping[part]) {
                        let displayText = part + ': ' + generalStudiesMapping[part];
                        
                        // Add date note based on code length
                        if (part.length === 4) {
                            displayText += ' (for students starting an ASU degree fall 2024 or later)';
                        } else {
                            displayText += ' (for students that started an ASU degree fall 2023 or earlier)';
                        }
                        
                        generalStudiesList.push(displayText);
                    } else {
                        // If not found in mapping, just add the original code
                        generalStudiesList.push(part);
                    }
                });
            });
            
            // Remove duplicates from the final list
            generalStudiesList = [...new Set(generalStudiesList)];
            
            if (generalStudiesList.length > 0) {
                finalHtml += '<div class="mb-2"><strong>Meets general studies requirement:</strong></div>';
                finalHtml += '<ul class="mb-1">';
                generalStudiesList.forEach(item => {
                    finalHtml += '<li>' + item + '</li>';
                });
                finalHtml += '</ul>';
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Only show "View course details page" link for single courses (since multiple courses already have individual links)
    if (courses && courses.length === 1) {
        const course = courses[0];
        const catalogUrl = 'https://catalog.apps.asu.edu/catalog/courses/courselist?subject=' + course.subject + '&catalogNbr=' + course.catalogNumber;
        finalHtml += '<div class="mb-2"><a href="' + catalogUrl + '" target="_blank">View course details page<span class="sr-only"> (opens in new window)</span></a></div>';
    }
    
    loadingDiv.innerHTML = finalHtml;
}

// Load general studies descriptions for static content on page load
// REMOVED: Replaced with simple hardcoded message

// Initialize the overview content on page load
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const contentContainer = document.getElementById('overview-content');
    const toggleLink = document.getElementById('overview-toggle');
	const overviewHeading = document.getElementById('overview-heading');
    
    // Process links in major description content to add new tab behavior and analytics tracking
    if (contentContainer) {
        // Find all anchor tags with href attributes in the major description
        const links = contentContainer.querySelectorAll('a[href]');
        
        // Get the heading text for tracking
        const headingText = overviewHeading ? overviewHeading.textContent.trim() : 'overview';
        
        // Process each link to add new tab behavior and onclick tracking
        links.forEach(function(link) {
            // Add target="_blank" to make links open in new tabs
            // Only add if it doesn't already have a target attribute to avoid conflicts
            if (!link.hasAttribute('target')) {
                link.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
                
                // Add accessibility text for screen readers
                const srText = link.querySelector('.sr-only');
                if (!srText) {
                    const screenReaderSpan = document.createElement('span');
                    screenReaderSpan.className = 'sr-only';
                    screenReaderSpan.textContent = ' (opens in new window)';
                    link.appendChild(screenReaderSpan);
                }
            }
            
            // Add onclick attribute for analytics tracking instead of event listener
            if (!link.hasAttribute('onclick')) {
                link.setAttribute('onclick', "trackLink('" + headingText + "', '')");
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Existing toggle functionality for show more/less
    if (contentContainer && toggleLink) {
        console.log('Overview initialization: Elements found');
        
        // Check if content is longer than the threshold to show Read more.
        // Threshold should be slightly above the collapsed height (200px) so the button
        // appears when there's meaningful hidden content.
        const fullHeight = contentContainer.scrollHeight;
        const showButtonThreshold = 220;
        
        console.log('Content height:', fullHeight, 'Show button threshold:', showButtonThreshold);
        
        if (fullHeight > showButtonThreshold) {
            // Content is long enough to need read more
            contentContainer.classList.add('collapsed');
            toggleLink.textContent = 'Read more';
            toggleLink.style.display = 'inline-block';
            toggleLink.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        } else {
            // Content is short, hide the toggle
            toggleLink.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
});


// Career table show more/less toggle
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const careerTableContainer = document.getElementById('career-table-container');
	const careerToggleButton = document.getElementById('career-table-toggle');
	
	if (careerTableContainer && careerToggleButton && window.innerWidth <= 768) {
		// Check if content is longer than collapsed height (900px)
		const fullHeight = careerTableContainer.scrollHeight;
		const collapsedHeight = 900;
		
		if (fullHeight > collapsedHeight) {
			// Content is long enough to need show more
			careerTableContainer.classList.add('collapsed');
			careerToggleButton.style.display = 'block';
		} else {
			// Content is short, no need for show more
			careerToggleButton.style.display = 'none';
		}
		
		careerToggleButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
			if (careerTableContainer.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
				// Expanding: set max-height to scrollHeight
				careerTableContainer.style.maxHeight = careerTableContainer.scrollHeight + 'px';
				careerTableContainer.classList.remove('collapsed');
				careerToggleButton.textContent = 'Show less';
				careerToggleButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
				
				// DataLayer tracking for expanding
				if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
					dataLayer.push({
						"event": "collapse",
						"action": "open",
						"name": "onclick",
						"type": "click",
						"region": "main content",
						"section": "build the career and future you want",
						"text": "show more"
					});
				}
			} else {
				// Collapsing: set to scrollHeight first, then to collapsed height
				careerTableContainer.style.maxHeight = careerTableContainer.scrollHeight + 'px';
				setTimeout(() => {
					careerTableContainer.classList.add('collapsed');
					careerTableContainer.style.maxHeight = '';
				}, 10);
				careerToggleButton.textContent = 'Show more';
				careerToggleButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
				
				// DataLayer tracking for collapsing
				if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
					dataLayer.push({
						"event": "collapse",
						"action": "close",
						"name": "onclick",
						"type": "click",
						"region": "main content",
						"section": "build the career and future you want",
						"text": "show less"
					});
				}
			}
		});
		
		// Set initial aria-expanded state
		if (careerTableContainer.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
			careerToggleButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
		}
	}
});
