// Animate career opportunities list items
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const careerOppsList = document.querySelector('.career-opps-list ul');
	
	if (!careerOppsList) return;
	
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				const listItems = entry.target.querySelectorAll('li');
				listItems.forEach((item, index) => {
					setTimeout(() => {
						item.classList.add('item-visible');
					}, (listItems.length - 1 - index) * 100);
				});
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -10% 0px'
	});
	
	observer.observe(careerOppsList);
});

// Animate course items when they scroll into view
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const courseYearContents = document.querySelectorAll('.course-year-content');
	
	if (courseYearContents.length === 0) return;
	
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				const courseItems = entry.target.querySelectorAll('.course-item');
				const isOpen = entry.target.classList.contains('open');
				
				if (isOpen) {
					// If accordion is open, animate with stagger (visible animation)
					courseItems.forEach((item, index) => {
						setTimeout(() => {
							item.classList.add('animate');
						}, (courseItems.length - 1 - index) * 100);
					});
				} else {
					// If accordion is closed, immediately add animate class (invisible pre-animation)
					courseItems.forEach(item => {
						item.classList.add('animate');
					});
				}
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -10% 0px'
	});
	
	// Observe all course year content containers
	courseYearContents.forEach(content => {
		observer.observe(content);
	});
});

// Accordion collapse functionality
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const accordionButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="collapse"]');
	
	accordionButtons.forEach(button => {
		function handleToggle(e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			
			const targetId = button.getAttribute('data-bs-target');
			const targetElement = document.querySelector(targetId);
			const parentAccordion = button.getAttribute('data-bs-parent');
			
			if (!targetElement) return;
			
			// If parent accordion exists, close other items (except for Degree Details accordions)
			if (parentAccordion && parentAccordion !== '#admissions-accordion' && parentAccordion !== '#admissions-accordion-extra') {
				const parent = document.querySelector(parentAccordion);
				if (parent) {
					const allCollapses = parent.querySelectorAll('.accordion-collapse');
					allCollapses.forEach(collapse => {
						if (collapse !== targetElement && collapse.classList.contains('show')) {
							const collapseButton = parent.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#' + collapse.id + '"]');
							collapseElement(collapse, collapseButton);
						}
					});
				}
			}
			
			// Toggle current item
			if (targetElement.classList.contains('show')) {
				collapseElement(targetElement, button);
			} else {
				expandElement(targetElement, button);
			}
		}
		
		// Click event
		button.addEventListener('click', handleToggle);
		
		// Keyboard event for Enter and Spacebar
		button.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
			if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
				handleToggle(e);
			}
		});
		
		// Set initial aria-label
		const isExpanded = button.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
		const buttonText = button.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
		if (!button.hasAttribute('aria-label')) {
			button.setAttribute('aria-label', buttonText + (isExpanded ? ', expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.' : ', collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.'));
		}
	});
	
	function expandElement(element, button) {
		// Update button state immediately for icon rotation
		if (button) {
			button.classList.remove('collapsed');
			button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
			// Update aria-label to communicate state
			const buttonText = button.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
			button.setAttribute('aria-label', buttonText + ', expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.');
		}
		
		element.classList.remove('collapse');
		element.classList.add('collapsing');
		element.style.height = '0';
		
		const height = element.scrollHeight;
		
		setTimeout(() => {
			element.style.height = height + 'px';
		}, 10);
		
		setTimeout(() => {
			element.classList.remove('collapsing');
			element.classList.add('collapse', 'show');
			element.style.height = '';
		}, 350);
	}
	
	function collapseElement(element, button) {
		// Update button state immediately for icon rotation
		if (button) {
			button.classList.add('collapsed');
			button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
			// Update aria-label to communicate state
			const buttonText = button.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
			button.setAttribute('aria-label', buttonText + ', collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.');
		}
		
		element.style.height = element.scrollHeight + 'px';
		
		setTimeout(() => {
			element.classList.add('collapsing');
			element.classList.remove('collapse', 'show');
			element.style.height = '0';
		}, 10);
		
		setTimeout(() => {
			element.classList.remove('collapsing');
			element.classList.add('collapse');
			element.style.height = '';
		}, 350);
	}
});

// Campus URL mapping function (copied from programs.js)
function getCampusUrl(campusCode, program) {
    var campus = campusCode.toUpperCase();
    
    if (campus == "TEMPE")
        return "https://campus.asu.edu/tempe";
    else if (campus == "POLY")
        return "https://campus.asu.edu/polytechnic";
    else if (campus == "WEST")
        return "https://campus.asu.edu/west-valley";
    else if (campus == "DTPHX")
        return "https://campus.asu.edu/downtown-phoenix";
    else if (campus == "EAC")
        return "https://admission.asu.edu/transfer/thegilavalley";
    else if (campus == "ONLNE")
        return "https://asuonline.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "CALHC")
        return "https://havasu.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "TUCSN" && program == "undergrad")
        return "https://socialwork.asu.edu/tucson";
    else if (campus == "TUCSN" && program == "graduate")
        return "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "MXCTY")
        return "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "AWC" && program == "undergrad")
        return "https://admission.asu.edu/transfer/asu-yuma";
    else if (campus == "AWC" && program == "graduate")
        return "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "WASHD")
        return "https://washingtondc.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "LOSAN")
        return "https://california.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "MESA")
        return "https://cfo.asu.edu/MesaCityCenter";
    else if (campus == "HAINA")
        return "https://global.asu.edu/what-we-do/hainan";
    else if (campus == "SYNC")
        return "https://provost.asu.edu/sync/students";
    
    return "#"; // default fallback
}

// Campus URL mapping function (copied from programs.js)
function openCampusLink(campusCode, program) {
    var campus = campusCode.toUpperCase();
    var url = '#'; // default fallback
    
    if (campus == "TEMPE")
        url = "https://campus.asu.edu/tempe";
    else if (campus == "POLY")
        url = "https://campus.asu.edu/polytechnic";
    else if (campus == "WEST")
        url = "https://campus.asu.edu/west-valley";
    else if (campus == "DTPHX")
        url = "https://campus.asu.edu/downtown-phoenix";
    else if (campus == "EAC")
        url = "https://admission.asu.edu/transfer/thegilavalley";
    else if (campus == "ONLNE")
        url = "https://asuonline.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "CALHC")
        url = "https://havasu.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "TUCSN" && program == "undergrad")
        url = "https://socialwork.asu.edu/tucson";
    else if (campus == "TUCSN" && program == "graduate")
        url = "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "MXCTY")
        url = "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "AWC" && program == "undergrad")
        url = "https://admission.asu.edu/transfer/asu-yuma";
    else if (campus == "AWC" && program == "graduate")
        url = "https://admission.asu.edu/contact/graduate";
    else if (campus == "WASHD")
        url = "https://washingtondc.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "LOSAN")
        url = "https://california.asu.edu";
    else if (campus == "MESA")
        url = "https://cfo.asu.edu/MesaCityCenter";
    else if (campus == "HAINA")
        url = "https://global.asu.edu/what-we-do/hainan";
    else if (campus == "SYNC")
        url = "https://provost.asu.edu/sync/students";
    
    // Open URL in new tab
    window.open(url, '_blank');
}

// checkRadiosSubplan function (copied from ds.js)
function checkRadiosSubplan() {
    var selectedInput = document.querySelector('input[name="subPlan"]:checked');

    // Get the associated span text (e.g., "Spanish, BA")
    var spanText = '';
    if (selectedInput) {
        var td = selectedInput.closest('td');
        var span = td.querySelector('span');
        if (span) {
            spanText = span.textContent.trim();
        }
    }

    // Add dataLayer tracking for show curriculum action
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "link",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "internal link",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "curriculum",
            "text": "show curriculum"
        });
    }

    document.getElementById("subPlanForm").submit();
}

// showShowMajorMapBtn function (copied from ds.js)
function showShowMajorMapBtn() {
    // Add dataLayer tracking for subplan selection
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        // Get the selected subplan information
        var selectedInput = document.querySelector('input[name="subPlan"]:checked');
        var spanText = '';
        
        if (selectedInput) {
            var td = selectedInput.closest('td');
            var span = td.querySelector('span');
            if (span) {
                spanText = span.textContent.trim();
            }
        }
        
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "select",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "curriculum",
            "text": spanText.toLowerCase()
        });
    }
    
    $("#showMajorMapBtn").show();
}

function checkAuditRadios(checkSelection) {
  if (checkSelection) {
    // Track the Run Audit button click
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
      dataLayer.push({
        "event": "form",
        "action": "submit",
        "name": "onclick",
        "type": "click",
        "region": "main content",
        "section": "what if",
        "text": "run audit"
      });
    }
    
    var selected = false;
    $("#whatIfForm")
      .find("input")
      .each(function () {
        if ($(this).is(":checked") && $(this).val() != "REQUIREMENTS") {
          selected = true;
        }
      });
    if (!selected) {
      showMessage();
      return;
    } else {
      document.getElementById("whatIfForm").submit();
      document.getElementById("cluetip").style.display = "none";
    }
  }
}

// Career section scroll animation
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const careersSection = document.querySelector('#careers');
	if (!careersSection) return;
	
	// Add pre-animate class to table rows for progressive enhancement
	const careerTable = careersSection.querySelector('.career-table tbody');
	if (careerTable) {
		const rows = careerTable.querySelectorAll('tr');
		rows.forEach(row => row.classList.add('pre-animate'));
	}
	
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				// Start background animations
				const careerHero = entry.target.querySelector('.career-hero');
				if (careerHero) {
					careerHero.classList.add('animate-backgrounds');
				}
				
				// Animate table rows with stagger
				const careerTable = entry.target.querySelector('.career-table tbody');
				if (careerTable) {
					const rows = careerTable.querySelectorAll('tr');
					rows.forEach((row, index) => {
						setTimeout(() => {
							row.classList.add('row-visible');
						}, index * 100);
					});
				}
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -45% 0px'
	});
	
	observer.observe(careersSection);
});

// Cost section scroll animation
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const costSection = document.querySelector('#cost');
	if (!costSection) return;
	
	// Add pre-animate class to enable the animation (progressive enhancement)
	const costHero = costSection.querySelector('.cost-hero');
	if (costHero) costHero.classList.add('pre-animate');
	
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				const costHero = entry.target.querySelector('.cost-hero');
				if (costHero) {
					costHero.classList.add('animate-cost');
				}
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -45% 0px'
	});
	
	observer.observe(costSection);
});

// Degree overview card animation on scroll
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const degreeOverviewCard = document.querySelector('.degree-overview-card');
	if (!degreeOverviewCard) return;
	
	// Add pre-animate class to enable the fade-in animation (progressive enhancement)
	degreeOverviewCard.classList.add('pre-animate');
	
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				// Add animation class when card comes into view
				entry.target.classList.add('animate');
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -25% 0px'
	});
	
	observer.observe(degreeOverviewCard);
});

// Courses decorative image stack: fade in + slide up when scrolled into view.
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const stacks = document.querySelectorAll('.courses-books-stack');
	if (!stacks.length) return;

	const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {
				entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
				observer.unobserve(entry.target);
			}
		});
	}, {
		// Shrink the viewport's bottom by 40% so the stack must scroll further up
		// before it counts as "in view" - the animation triggers when the section is
		// higher in the viewport rather than as soon as its top edge appears.
		rootMargin: '0px 0px -40% 0px'
	});

	stacks.forEach(stack => observer.observe(stack));
});

// Sticky nav show/hide on scroll (mobile and desktop)
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const heroSection = document.querySelector('.hero-section');
	const tabNav = document.querySelector('.tab-nav');
	const header = document.querySelector('#asu-header') || document.querySelector('header') || document.querySelector('.header');
	// Choose the scroll trigger. Prefer a hero CTA button, but MANY candidates can be
	// hidden (the "Request info" button is style="display:none" until the RFI React
	// form confirms it renders; the Apply button may not exist; the favorites button
	// only shows when active-in-major). A display:none element has a zero-size
	// getBoundingClientRect (top === 0), which would make the "reached the top" check
	// fire immediately - the root cause of the header jumping to the top after only a
	// tiny scroll. So skip any hidden/zero-size candidate and fall back to a container
	// that is always rendered with real height (the hero buttons row, then the hero
	// content, then the hero section).
	function isRendered(el) {
		// offsetParent is null for display:none; also guard against zero-height boxes.
		return el && el.offsetParent !== null && el.getBoundingClientRect().height > 0;
	}
	const triggerCandidates = [
		document.querySelector('.hero-buttons .btn-primary'),
		document.querySelector('.hero-buttons .btn'),
		document.querySelector('.hero-buttons'),
		document.querySelector('.program-hero-content'),
		heroSection
	];
	const triggerElement = triggerCandidates.find(isRendered) || heroSection;
	
	if (heroSection && tabNav && header && triggerElement) {
		// Decide hide/show from the trigger's VIEWPORT position, not an absolute
		// document position. getBoundingClientRect().top is relative to the current
		// viewport and is immune to what's above the trigger in the document - which
		// matters because the ASU header renders asynchronously and, once hidden, we
		// shift content up via body padding. Absolute-position math raced with those
		// shifts and produced a too-small threshold, hiding the header after only a
		// tiny scroll. "Trigger reaches the top of the screen" is simply rect.top <= 0.
		let headerHidden = false;

		function showHeader() {
			headerHidden = false;
			tabNav.classList.remove('show-sticky');
			header.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
			header.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
			if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
				document.body.style.paddingTop = '';
				document.body.style.transition = 'padding-top 0.3s ease';
			}
		}

		function hideHeader() {
			headerHidden = true;
			tabNav.classList.add('show-sticky');
			header.style.transform = 'translateY(-100%)';
			header.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
			if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
				document.body.style.paddingTop = '0';
				document.body.style.transition = 'padding-top 0.3s ease';
			}
		}

		window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
			const scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;

			// Hard guarantee: at (or above) the very top, always show the header.
			if (scrollTop <= 0) {
				if (headerHidden) showHeader();
				return;
			}

			// Hide once the trigger has scrolled up to (or past) the top of the
			// viewport; show again when it comes back below the top.
			const triggerViewportTop = triggerElement.getBoundingClientRect().top;

			if (triggerViewportTop <= 0) {
				if (!headerHidden) hideHeader();
			} else {
				if (headerHidden) showHeader();
			}
		});
	}
});

// Copy plan code to clipboard
function copyPlanCode(button) {
    var planCode = button.getAttribute('data-plan-code');
    if (!planCode) return;

    // Track the copy action
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "link",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "internal link",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "quick facts",
            "text": "copy plan code icon"
        });
    }

    function showCopied() {
        button.dataset.copied = 'true';
        setPlanCodeState(button, 'copied');
        // Announce to screen readers
        var statusEl = document.getElementById('copy-plan-code-status');
        if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'Plan code copied to clipboard';
        setTimeout(function () {
            button.dataset.copied = 'false';
            // Revert to hover text if still hovered, otherwise the default plan code
            setPlanCodeState(button, button.matches(':hover') ? 'hover' : 'default');
            if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = '';
        }, 2000);
    }

    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(planCode).then(showCopied).catch(function () {
            fallbackCopyText(planCode);
            showCopied();
        });
    } else {
        fallbackCopyText(planCode);
        showCopied();
    }
}

// Swap the plan code button label on hover (ignored while the COPIED! state is showing)
function hoverPlanCode(button, entering) {
    if (button.dataset.copied === 'true') return;
    setPlanCodeState(button, entering ? 'hover' : 'default');
}

// Show one stacked label variant and hide the others; CSS handles the opacity crossfade.
// All variants share one grid cell, so the button width stays locked to the widest variant.
function setPlanCodeState(button, state) {
    var variants = button.querySelectorAll('.copy-plan-code-text');
    for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        variants[i].classList.toggle('is-visible', variants[i].getAttribute('data-state') === state);
    }
}

// Fallback copy for non-secure contexts / older browsers where navigator.clipboard is unavailable
function fallbackCopyText(text) {
    var textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
    textarea.value = text;
    textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
    textarea.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
    textarea.focus();
    textarea.select();
    try {
        document.execCommand('copy');
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Copy failed:', e);
    }
    document.body.removeChild(textarea);
}

// Mobile navigation dropdown toggle
function toggleMobileNav() {
    const navMenu = document.getElementById('mobile-nav-menu');
    const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-toggle');
    
    if (navMenu && toggleButton) {
        // Determine action based on current state (before toggle)
        const isCurrentlyOpen = toggleButton.classList.contains('active');
        const action = isCurrentlyOpen ? 'close' : 'open';
        
        // Toggle the navigation
        navMenu.classList.toggle('show');
        toggleButton.classList.toggle('active');
        
        // Track the collapse action
        const dataLayerObj = {
            "event": "collapse",
            "action": action,
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "click",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "sticky navbar",
            "text": "menu button mobile"
        };
        
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push(dataLayerObj);
        }
    }
}

// Close mobile nav when clicking on a link
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#mobile-nav-menu a');
    navLinks.forEach(link => {
        link.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const navMenu = document.getElementById('mobile-nav-menu');
            const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.mobile-nav-toggle');
            if (navMenu && toggleButton) {
                navMenu.classList.remove('show');
                toggleButton.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });
    });
});

// Generic Modal functions
let lastFocusedElement = null; // Store the element that triggered the modal

function openModal(modalId, ctaText) {
    const modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
    if (modal) {
        // Store the currently focused element so we can return focus when modal closes
        lastFocusedElement = document.activeElement;
        
        // Calculate scrollbar width and add padding to prevent content jump
        const scrollbarWidth = window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth;
        
        modal.style.display = 'flex';
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; // Prevent background scrolling
        
        // Only add padding if there's a scrollbar AND we're on desktop (not mobile/tablet)
        // Mobile devices typically have overlay scrollbars that don't take up space
        const isDesktop = window.innerWidth > 991; // Matches the mobile media query breakpoint
        if (scrollbarWidth > 0 && isDesktop) {
            document.body.style.paddingRight = scrollbarWidth + 'px'; // Compensate for scrollbar
        }
        
        // Check if the clicked element is from sticky navigation
        const isFromStickyNav = lastFocusedElement && (
            lastFocusedElement.closest('.sticky-nav-buttons') !== null ||
            lastFocusedElement.classList.contains('stick-nav-button')
        );
        
        // Determine section based on source
        const section = isFromStickyNav ? "sticky navbar" : globalProgramName;
        
        // Add dataLayer tracking for applyNowModal
        if (modalId === 'applyNowModal') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": section,
                // CTA varies: apply now / add certificate / add minor
                "text": ctaText ? ctaText.toLowerCase() : "apply now"
            });
            loadApplyNowContent();
        }
        
        // Load dynamic content for Request Info modal
        if (modalId === 'requestInfoModal') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": section,
                "text": "request info"
            });
            loadRequestInfoContent();
        }
        
        // Add dataLayer tracking for stemOptModal
        if (modalId === 'stemOptModal') {
            // Get text from stem-card-title element
            const stemCardTitle = document.querySelector('.stem-card-title');
            const stemOptText = stemCardTitle ? stemCardTitle.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() : 'stem-opt extension eligible';
            
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "quick facts",
                "text": stemOptText
            });
        }
        
        // Add dataLayer tracking for accreditationModal
        if (modalId === 'accreditationModal') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "quick facts",
                "text": "accreditation"
            });
        }
        
        // Load dynamic content for Curriculum Archive modal
        if (modalId === 'curriculumArchiveModal') {
            // Get the button text dynamically from whichever button triggered the modal
            var archiveBtn = document.querySelector('[data-testid="btn-curriculum-archive"]');
            var archiveBtnText = archiveBtn ? archiveBtn.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase() : 'view curriculum archives';
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "courses",
                "text": archiveBtnText
            });
            loadCurriculumArchiveContent();
        }
        
        // Load dynamic content for Checksheet modal
        if (modalId === 'checksheetModal') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "link",
                "action": "click",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "internal link",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "courses",
                "text": "view curriculum checksheet"
            });
            loadChecksheetContent();
        }
        
        // Load dynamic content for What If modal
        if (modalId === 'whatIfModal') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "open",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "courses",
                "text": "what if"
            });
            loadWhatIfContent();
        }
        
        // Trigger animation after display is set
        setTimeout(() => {
            modal.classList.add('show');
            
            // Move focus into the modal for accessibility
            // Find the first focusable element (prefer the close button)
            const closeButton = modal.querySelector('.modal-back');
            if (closeButton) {
                closeButton.focus();
            } else {
                // Fallback: focus the modal itself or first focusable element
                const focusableElements = modal.querySelectorAll(
                    'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
                );
                if (focusableElements.length > 0) {
                    focusableElements[0].focus();
                } else {
                    modal.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
                    modal.focus();
                }
            }
            
            // Setup focus trapping
            setupFocusTrap(modal);
        }, 10);
    }
}

// Focus trap implementation
function setupFocusTrap(modal) {
    // Remove any existing trap listener
    if (modal.focusTrapListener) {
        document.removeEventListener('keydown', modal.focusTrapListener);
    }
    
    // Create the trap listener - attach to document to catch all tab events
    modal.focusTrapListener = function(e) {
        // Only handle if modal is visible
        if (modal.style.display === 'none') return;
        
        if (e.key === 'Tab') {
            const focusableElements = modal.querySelectorAll(
                'button:not([disabled]), [href]:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"]):not([disabled])'
            );
            
            // Filter to only include visible elements (not hidden by display:none or visibility:hidden)
            const focusableArray = Array.from(focusableElements).filter(el => {
                return el.offsetParent !== null || el === document.activeElement;
            });
            
            // If no focusable elements, prevent all tabbing
            if (focusableArray.length === 0) {
                e.preventDefault();
                return;
            }
            
            const firstElement = focusableArray[0];
            const lastElement = focusableArray[focusableArray.length - 1];
            
            // Always prevent default Tab behavior when modal is open
            // We'll manually control focus
            e.preventDefault();
            
            // Find current element's index in the focusable elements array
            const currentIndex = focusableArray.indexOf(document.activeElement);
            
            if (e.shiftKey) {
                // Shift + Tab (backwards)
                if (currentIndex <= 0 || currentIndex === -1) {
                    // At first element, not found, or before first - wrap to last
                    lastElement.focus();
                } else {
                    // Go to previous focusable element
                    focusableArray[currentIndex - 1].focus();
                }
            } else {
                // Tab (forward)
                if (currentIndex === -1 || currentIndex >= focusableArray.length - 1) {
                    // Not found or at last element - wrap to first
                    firstElement.focus();
                } else {
                    // Go to next focusable element
                    focusableArray[currentIndex + 1].focus();
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Also handle Escape key to close modal
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            const modalId = modal.id;
            if (modalId) {
                closeModal(modalId);
            }
        }
    };
    
    // Attach to document to catch all tab events
    document.addEventListener('keydown', modal.focusTrapListener);
}

function closeModal(modalId) {
    const modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
    if (modal) {
        // Remove focus trap listener from document
        if (modal.focusTrapListener) {
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', modal.focusTrapListener);
            modal.focusTrapListener = null;
        }
        
        modal.classList.remove('show');

        // Remove openWhatIf parameter from URL when closing whatIfModal
        if (modalId === 'whatIfModal' && window.location.search.indexOf('openWhatIf=true') > -1) {
            const url = new URL(window.location.href);
            url.searchParams.delete('openWhatIf');
            history.replaceState(null, '', url.toString());
        }
        
        // Add dataLayer tracking for modal close events
        // Mapping of modal IDs to their corresponding section names for analytics
        const modalSections = {
            'applyNowModal': 'apply now',
            'requestInfoModal': 'request information',
            'stemOptModal': 'stem-opt extension for f-1 international students',
            'accreditationModal': 'accreditation',
            'curriculumArchiveModal': 'curriculum archive',
            'whatIfModal': 'what if'
        };
        
        if (modalSections[modalId]) {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "modal",
                "action": "close",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "click",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": modalSections[modalId],
                "text": "back"
            });
        }
        
        // Hide modal after animation completes
        setTimeout(() => {
            modal.style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = 'auto'; // Restore scrolling
            document.body.style.paddingRight = ''; // Remove scrollbar compensation
            
            // Return focus to the element that triggered the modal
            if (lastFocusedElement && lastFocusedElement.focus) {
                lastFocusedElement.focus();
                lastFocusedElement = null;
            }
        }, 800);
    }
}

// Load dynamic content for Apply Now modal
function loadApplyNowContent() {
    const loadingDiv = document.getElementById('apply-now-loading');
    const contentDiv = document.getElementById('apply-now-content');
    
    // Show loading, hide content
    loadingDiv.style.display = 'block';
    contentDiv.style.display = 'none';
    
    // For certificates/minors, use the dedicated endpoint that shows a simple
    // redirect message and button instead of the radio-button apply flow.
    const isCert = false;
    var apiUrl;
    if (isCert) {
        
            
            
                apiUrl = '/tooltipApplyMinor/BAACCBS';
            
        
    } else {
        // Regular degree apply flow with radio buttons
        const hasConcurrentDegrees = false;
        const concurrentParam = hasConcurrentDegrees ? '/true' : '/false';
        apiUrl = '/tooltipapply/ASU00/BAACCBS/UGBA/undergrad' + concurrentParam;
    }
    fetch(apiUrl)
        .then(response => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
            }
            return response.text();
        })
        .then(data => {
            // Hide loading, show content
            loadingDiv.style.display = 'none';
            contentDiv.style.display = 'block';
            
            // Add title and dynamic content
            contentDiv.innerHTML = '<h2 style="margin-bottom: ' + (isCert ? '2rem' : '0px') + ';">Apply now</h2>' + data;
            
            // Initialize radio button functionality after content is loaded (only for regular degrees)
            if (!isCert) {
                initializeApplyFormRadios();
            }
            
            // Add dataLayer tracking to the action button inside the modal (Add Minor/Add Certificate/Apply)
            var modalBtn = contentDiv.querySelector('.btn-maroon, .btn-primary');
            if (modalBtn && !modalBtn.getAttribute('onclick')) {
                var btnText = modalBtn.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
                var sectionName = 'apply now';
                modalBtn.setAttribute('onclick', "if(typeof dataLayer!=='undefined'){dataLayer.push({'event':'link','action':'click','name':'onclick','type':'internal link','region':'main content','section':'" + sectionName + "','text':'" + btnText + "'});}");
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading apply now content:', error);
            loadingDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #d32f2f;">Error loading application information. Please try again later.</p>';
        });
}

// Initialize radio button functionality for Apply Now modal
function initializeApplyFormRadios() {
    // Wrap each radio input and its adjacent text/span in a <label> so clicking
    // the text selects the radio. The server-generated HTML structure is:
    //   <div><input type="radio" .../><span class="campusDescr">Text</span></div>
    document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[type="radio"]').forEach(function(radio) {
        // Skip if already inside a <label>
        if (radio.closest('label')) return;

        var parent = radio.parentNode;
        var label = document.createElement('label');
        label.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        label.style.display = 'block';
        label.style.fontWeight = 'normal';
        label.style.fontSize = 'inherit';
        label.style.marginBottom = '0.5rem';

        // Insert the label before the radio, then move the radio into it
        parent.insertBefore(label, radio);
        label.appendChild(radio);

        // Move all subsequent siblings (text nodes, spans, br) into the label
        // until we hit another input, div, or the end
        while (label.nextSibling) {
            var sibling = label.nextSibling;
            // Stop at block elements or other inputs
            if (sibling.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
                var tag = sibling.tagName.toLowerCase();
                if (tag === 'input' || tag === 'div' || tag === 'table' || tag === 'a' || tag === 'button') break;
            }
            label.appendChild(sibling);
        }
    });

    // Hide all sections except location by default
    hideAllFormSections();
    
    // Show campus radio buttons for the current plan if there is no concurrent degrees
    if (true) {
		// Show the location (campus) selection
		document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseLocation"]').forEach(el => {
			el.style.display = "block";
		});
        const currentPlan = 'BAACCBS';
        document.querySelectorAll('[id^="plan_' + currentPlan + '"]').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = "block";
            el.classList.remove("no-display");
        });
    }
    
    // Remove onclick attributes from all radio buttons to prevent duplicate calls
    document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[type="radio"]').forEach(radio => {
        radio.removeAttribute('onclick');
    });
    
    // Add event listeners to plan radio buttons (if any)
    const planRadios = document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[name="plan"]');
    planRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (this.checked) {
                checkPlanRadios(this.value);
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Add event listeners to campus radio buttons
    const campusRadios = document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[name="campus"]');
    campusRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (this.checked) {
                const plan = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="plan"]:checked')?.value || 'BAACCBS';
                checkCampusRadios(plan, this.value);
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Add event listeners to term radio buttons
    const termRadios = document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[name="term"]');
    termRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (this.checked) {
                const plan = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="plan"]:checked')?.value || 'BAACCBS';
                const campus = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="campus"]:checked')?.value;
                if (campus) {
                    // Parse term and session from this.value
                    // Format: "ASU00,ESCSEBS-2264-undergrad-TEMPE-REG"
                    const valueParts = this.value.split('-');
                    let term = '';
                    let session = '';
                    
                    if (valueParts.length >= 5) {
                        term = valueParts[1]; // "2264"
                        session = valueParts[4]; // "REG"
                    }
                    
                    checkTermRadios(plan, campus, term, session);
                }
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Add event listeners to subplan radio buttons
    const subplanRadios = document.querySelectorAll('#applyForm input[name="subplan"]');
    subplanRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (this.checked) {
                // Get subplan description for dataLayer
                let subplanDescr = '';
                const subplanContainer = this.closest('div');
                if (subplanContainer) {
                    const subplanDescrSpan = subplanContainer.querySelector('.subplanDescr');
                    if (subplanDescrSpan) {
                        subplanDescr = subplanDescrSpan.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
                    }
                }
                
                // GTM tracking (if dataLayer exists)
                if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
                    dataLayer.push({
                        "event": "select",
                        "action": "click",
                        "name": "onclick",
                        "type": "checkbox",
                        "region": "main content",
                        "section": "choose a subplan",
                        "text": subplanDescr || "subplan not found"
                    });
                }
                
                // Show the start application button when subplan is selected
                const startBtn = document.getElementById('startApplicationBtn');
                if (startBtn) {
                    startBtn.style.display = 'block';
                }
            }
        });
    });
}

// Hide all form sections initially for apply now
function hideAllFormSections() {
    // Hide all dynamic sections
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="plan_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="campus_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('div[id^="term_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applysub"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseSubplan"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applyfor"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseLocation"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    
    // Hide start application button
    const startBtn = document.getElementById('startApplicationBtn');
    if (startBtn) {
        startBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

// Radio button functions from ds.js (adapted for modal)
function checkPlanRadios(plan) {
    const form = document.getElementById("applyForm");
    if (!form) return;
    
    // Get the selected plan radio button to extract the text
    const selectedPlanRadio = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="plan"]:checked');
    let planText = '';
    
    if (selectedPlanRadio) {
        // Look for the text in the same container (usually in a span or label)
        const planContainer = selectedPlanRadio.closest('div') || selectedPlanRadio.closest('td') || selectedPlanRadio.closest('label');
        if (planContainer) {
            // Get text content and clean it up
            planText = planContainer.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
            // Remove extra whitespace and line breaks
            planText = planText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
        }
    }
    
    // DataLayer tracking for plan selection
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined' && planText) {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "select",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "which degree would you like to apply to",
            "text": planText
        });
    }
    
    // Reset campus selections
    const selectionCampus = form.campus;
    if (selectionCampus && selectionCampus.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selectionCampus.length; i++) {
            if (selectionCampus[i].checked) {
                selectionCampus[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    // Reset term selections
    const selectionTerm = form.term;
    if (selectionTerm && selectionTerm.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selectionTerm.length; i++) {
            if (selectionTerm[i].checked) {
                selectionTerm[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Reset subplan selections
    const selectionSubplan = form.subplan;
    if (selectionSubplan && selectionSubplan.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selectionSubplan.length; i++) {
            if (selectionSubplan[i].checked) {
                selectionSubplan[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Hide/show relevant sections
    document.querySelectorAll('div[id^="term_"]').forEach(div => {
        div.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applysub"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="plan_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="campus_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applyfor"]').forEach(el => {
       el.style.display = "none";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="plan_' + plan + '"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "block";
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseLocation"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "block";
    });
	 // Hide start application button
    const startBtn = document.getElementById('startApplicationBtn');
    if (startBtn) {
        startBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

function checkCampusRadios(plan, campus) {
    const form = document.getElementById("applyForm");
    if (!form) return;

    // Get the selected campus radio button to extract campusDescr
    const selectedCampusRadio = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="campus"]:checked');
    let campusDescr = '';
    
    if (selectedCampusRadio) {
        // Look for the campusDescr span in the same container
        const campusContainer = selectedCampusRadio.closest('div');
        if (campusContainer) {
            const campusDescrSpan = campusContainer.querySelector('.campusDescr');
            if (campusDescrSpan) {
                campusDescr = campusDescrSpan.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
            }
        }
    }

    // GTM tracking (if dataLayer exists)
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "select",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "choose a location",
            "text": campusDescr || "campus not found"
        });
    }
    
    // Reset term selections
    const selection = form.term;
    if (selection && selection.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selection.length; i++) {
            if (selection[i].checked) {
                selection[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Reset subplan selections
    const selectionSubplan = form.subplan;
    if (selectionSubplan && selectionSubplan.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selectionSubplan.length; i++) {
            if (selectionSubplan[i].checked) {
                selectionSubplan[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Hide/show relevant sections
    document.querySelectorAll('div[id^="term_"]').forEach(div => {
        div.style.display = "none";
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applysub"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="campus_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="campus_' + plan + '_' + campus + '"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "block";
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseLocation"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "block";
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applyfor"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "block";
    });
    
    const startBtn = document.getElementById("startApplicationBtn");
    if (startBtn) {
        startBtn.style.display = "none";
    }
}

function checkTermRadios(plan, campus, term, session) {
    
    // Get the selected term radio button to extract termDescr
    const selectedTermRadio = document.querySelector('#applyForm input[name="term"]:checked');
    let termDescr = '';
    
    if (selectedTermRadio) {
        // Look for the termDescr span in the same container
        const termContainer = selectedTermRadio.closest('div');
        if (termContainer) {
            const termDescrSpan = termContainer.querySelector('.termDescr');
            if (termDescrSpan) {
                termDescr = termDescrSpan.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
            }
        }
    }
    
    // GTM tracking (if dataLayer exists)
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "select",
            "action": "click",
            "name": "onclick",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "region": "main content",
            "section": "choose a term",
            "text": termDescr || "term not found"
        });
    }

    
    const form = document.getElementById("applyForm");
    if (!form) return;
    
    // Reset subplan selections
    const selection = form.subplan;
    if (selection && selection.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < selection.length; i++) {
            if (selection[i].checked) {
                selection[i].checked = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    // Hide all term sections first
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="term_"]').forEach(el => {
        el.style.display = "none";
    });
    
    // Show the specific term container(s)
    const searchPattern = 'term_' + plan + '_' + campus + '_' + term + '_' + session;
    
    const termContainers = document.querySelectorAll('[id^="' + searchPattern + '"]');
    let hasSubplans = false;
    
    if (termContainers.length > 0) {
        // Show all matching term containers
        termContainers.forEach(container => {
            container.style.display = "block";
        });
        
        // Check all containers for subplan inputs (including hidden ones)
        termContainers.forEach(container => {
            const subplanInputs = container.querySelectorAll('input[name="subplan"]');
            console.log('Subplan inputs in', container.id, ':', subplanInputs.length);
            if (subplanInputs.length > 0) {
                hasSubplans = true;
            }
        });
        
        // Also check the entire form for subplan inputs that might be related to this term
        const allSubplanInputs = form.querySelectorAll('input[name="subplan"]');
        allSubplanInputs.forEach(input => {
            // Check if the input is within a container that matches our term pattern
            const parentContainer = input.closest('[id^="term_' + plan + '_' + campus + '_' + term + '"]');
            if (parentContainer) {
                console.log('Found related subplan in:', parentContainer.id);
                hasSubplans = true;
            }
        });
    }
    
    if (hasSubplans) {
        // Show subplan selection if subplans exist
        document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseSubplan"]').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = "block";
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applysub"]').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = "block";
        });
        
        // Hide start application button until subplan is selected
        const startBtn = document.getElementById("startApplicationBtn");
        if (startBtn) {
            startBtn.style.display = "none";
        }
    } else {
        // No subplans available, show start application button directly
        document.querySelectorAll('[id^="chooseSubplan"]').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = "none";
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('[id^="applysub"]').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = "none";
        });
        
        // Show start application button directly
        const startBtn = document.getElementById("startApplicationBtn");
        if (startBtn) {
            startBtn.style.display = "block";
        }
    }
}

	// GTM tracking (if dataLayer exists)
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            "event": "link",
			"action": "click",
			"name": "onclick",
			"type": "internal link",
			"region": "main content",
			"section": "apply now",
			"text": "start application"
        });
    }
	
	document.getElementById("applyForm").submit();
}

    const contentDiv = document.getElementById('whatif-content');
