// Load dynamic content for Checksheet modal
function loadChecksheetContent() {
    const loadingDiv = document.getElementById('checksheet-loading');
    const contentDiv = document.getElementById('checksheet-content');
    
    // Show loading, hide content
    loadingDiv.style.display = 'block';
    contentDiv.style.display = 'none';
    
    // Get the URL from the button's rel attribute
    const checksheetButton = document.querySelector('a[onclick*="checksheetModal"]');
    const apiUrl = checksheetButton ? checksheetButton.getAttribute('rel') : '';
    
    if (!apiUrl) {
        loadingDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #d32f2f;">Error: No URL found for loading checksheet options.</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    fetch(apiUrl)
        .then(response => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
            }
            return response.text();
        })
        .then(data => {
            // Hide loading, show content
            loadingDiv.style.display = 'none';
            contentDiv.style.display = 'block';
            
            // Add title and dynamic content
            contentDiv.innerHTML = '<h2>Select Checksheet Option</h2>' + data;
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error loading checksheet content:', error);
            loadingDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #d32f2f;">Error loading checksheet options. Please try again later.</p>';
        });
}

// Request info link function with dataLayer tracking
function openRequestInfo(url) {
    // Check if the clicked element is from sticky navigation
    const lastFocusedElement = document.activeElement;
    const isFromStickyNav = lastFocusedElement && (lastFocusedElement.closest('.sticky-nav-buttons') !== null ||
        lastFocusedElement.classList.contains('stick-nav-button'));
    
    // Determine section based on location
    const section = isFromStickyNav ? 'sticky navbar' : globalProgramName;
    
    // DataLayer tracking
    if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
        dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'link',
            'action': 'click',
            'name': 'onclick',
            'type': 'internal link',
            'region': 'main content',
            'section': section,
            'text': 'request info'
        });
    }
    
    // Open URL in new tab
    window.open(url, '_blank');
}

// Close modal when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const modalIds = ['applyNowModal', 'requestInfoModal', 'stemOptModal', 'accreditationModal', 'curriculumArchiveModal', 'checksheetModal', 'whatIfModal'];
    
    modalIds.forEach(modalId => {
        const modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
        if (modal) {
            modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                if (e.target === modal) {
                    closeModal(modalId);
                }
            });
        }
    });

    // Auto-open What If modal if redirected back after login
    if (window.location.search.indexOf('openWhatIf=true') > -1) {
        setTimeout(function() {
            openModal('whatIfModal');
        }, 500);
    }
});

// Career table sorting
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	// Track links inside PeopleSoft dynamic content sections (delegated)
	// Each entry: [elementId, sectionName, componentName (optional)]
	var dynamicSections = [
		['card-body-study-abroad', 'study abroad opportunities', 'explore expanded study opportunities'],
		['card-body-additional', 'additional degree-specific requirements', 'admission requirements'],
		['card-body-change-major', 'change of major requirements', 'admission requirements'],
		['enrollment-req-content', 'enrollment requirements', ''],
		['more-about-content', 'career opportunities', ''],
		['prof-licensure-content', 'professional licensure', 'accreditation']
	];
	
	dynamicSections.forEach(function(config) {
		var el = document.getElementById(config[0]);
		if (el) {
			el.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
				var link = e.target.closest('a');
				if (link && !link.getAttribute('onclick')) {
					var linkText = link.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
					var linkUrl = link.href || '';
					var linkType = 'internal link';
					if (linkUrl.startsWith('mailto:')) {
						linkType = 'external link';
					} else if (linkUrl && !linkUrl.includes('asu.edu')) {
						linkType = 'external link';
					}
					var dataObj = {
						"event": "link",
						"action": "click",
						"name": "onclick",
						"type": linkType,
						"region": "main content",
						"section": config[1],
						"text": linkText
					};
					if (config[2]) dataObj.component = config[2];
					if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
						dataLayer.push(dataObj);
					}
				}
			});
		}
	});
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const table = document.querySelector('.career-table');
	if (!table) return;
	
	const tbody = table.querySelector('tbody');
	const headers = table.querySelectorAll('th.sortable');
	
	// Store original order on page load
	const rows = Array.from(tbody.querySelectorAll('tr'));
	rows.forEach((row, index) => {
		row.dataset.originalIndex = index;
	});
	
	headers.forEach(header => {
		const handleSort = function() {
			const column = this.dataset.column;
			const currentSort = this.dataset.sort;
			
			// Determine next sort state
			let nextSort;
			if (currentSort === 'none') {
				nextSort = 'asc';
			} else if (currentSort === 'asc') {
				nextSort = 'desc';
			} else {
				nextSort = 'none';
			}
			
			// Reset all other headers
			headers.forEach(h => {
				if (h !== this) {
					h.dataset.sort = 'none';
					h.setAttribute('aria-sort', 'none');
				}
			});
			
			// Update current header
			this.dataset.sort = nextSort;
			
			// Update aria-sort attribute
			if (nextSort === 'none') {
				this.setAttribute('aria-sort', 'none');
			} else if (nextSort === 'asc') {
				this.setAttribute('aria-sort', 'ascending');
			} else {
				this.setAttribute('aria-sort', 'descending');
			}
			
			// Announce to screen readers
			const columnName = this.textContent.trim().split('\n')[0].trim();
			const announceText = nextSort === 'none' 
				? `Table sorted by original order` 
				: `Table sorted by , ending order`;
			const statusElement = document.getElementById('career-table-status');
			if (statusElement) {
				statusElement.textContent = announceText;
			}
			
			// Sort the rows
			const rows = Array.from(tbody.querySelectorAll('tr'));
			
			if (nextSort === 'none') {
				// Restore original order using stored indices
				rows.sort((a, b) => {
					return parseInt(a.dataset.originalIndex) - parseInt(b.dataset.originalIndex);
				});
			} else {
				rows.sort((a, b) => {
					let aValue, bValue;
					
					if (column === 'career') {
						aValue = a.cells[0].textContent.trim();
						bValue = b.cells[0].textContent.trim();
						return nextSort === 'asc' ? aValue.localeCompare(bValue) : bValue.localeCompare(aValue);
					} else if (column === 'growth') {
						// Extract just the numeric value, ignoring "Growth: " prefix and "%" suffix
						const aText = a.cells[1].textContent.trim();
						const bText = b.cells[1].textContent.trim();
						aValue = parseFloat(aText.replace(/Growth:\s*/, '').replace(/%.*/, ''));
						bValue = parseFloat(bText.replace(/Growth:\s*/, '').replace(/%.*/, ''));
						return nextSort === 'asc' ? aValue - bValue : bValue - aValue;
					} else if (column === 'salary') {
						// Extract just the numeric value, ignoring "Median Salary: " prefix
						const aText = a.cells[2].textContent.trim();
						const bText = b.cells[2].textContent.trim();
						aValue = parseFloat(aText.replace(/Median Salary:\s*/, '').replace(/[$,]/g, ''));
						bValue = parseFloat(bText.replace(/Median Salary:\s*/, '').replace(/[$,]/g, ''));
						return nextSort === 'asc' ? aValue - bValue : bValue - aValue;
					}
					return 0;
				});
			}
			
			// Re-append rows in sorted order
			rows.forEach(row => tbody.appendChild(row));
		};
		
		header.addEventListener('click', handleSort);
		
		// Add keyboard support for Enter and Space keys
		header.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
			if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
				e.preventDefault();
				handleSort.call(this);
			}
		});
	});
});

// SVG Heart Icon Functionality (compatible with ds.js)
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const heartIcons = document.querySelectorAll('svg.save');
	
	// Add simple CSS animations for heart icons
	const style = document.createElement('style');
	style.textContent = `
		/* Heart icon base styles */
		.save {
			transition: transform 0.2s ease;
			cursor: pointer;
		}
		
		/* Simple hover effect */
		.save:hover {
			transform: scale(1.1);
		}
		
		/* Simple click animations */
		.save.animate-favorite {
			animation: simpleBeat 0.4s ease;
		}
		
		.save.animate-unfavorite {
			animation: simpleShrink 0.3s ease;
		}
		
		/* Simple keyframe animations */
		@keyframes simpleBeat {
			0% { transform: scale(1); }
			50% { transform: scale(1.2); }
			100% { transform: scale(1); }
		}
		
		@keyframes simpleShrink {
			0% { transform: scale(1); }
			50% { transform: scale(0.9); }
			100% { transform: scale(1); }
		}
		
		/* Focus styles for accessibility */
		.save:focus {
			outline: 2px solid #0078d4;
			outline-offset: 2px;
		}
	`;
	document.head.appendChild(style);
	
	heartIcons.forEach(function(svg) {
		// Attach the click handler to the parent button so clicks anywhere on
		// the button trigger the save/unsave logic. Read data from the SVG child.
		var btn = svg.parentElement;
		if (!btn || btn.tagName.toLowerCase() !== 'button') return;
		btn.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
			const addUrl = svg.getAttribute('href');
			let acadPlan = svg.getAttribute('acadplancode');
			const heartPath = svg.querySelector('path:last-child'); // Get the heart path (not circle)
			const srOnlyElement = btn.querySelector('.sr-only');
			
			// If acadPlan is null/empty, extract from button id as fallback
			if (!acadPlan) {
				if (btn.id) {
					if (btn.id.startsWith('sticky-div')) {
						acadPlan = btn.id.substring(10); // Remove 'sticky-div' prefix
					} else if (btn.id.startsWith('div')) {
						acadPlan = btn.id.substring(3); // Remove 'div' prefix
					}
				}
			}
			
			const container = document.getElementById('div' + acadPlan);
			const stickyContainer = document.getElementById('sticky-div' + acadPlan);
			
			if (!heartPath) {
				return;
			}
			
			if (!acadPlan) {
				return;
			}
			
			let type = '';
			
			// Check current state by looking at fill attribute
			if (heartPath.getAttribute('fill') === 'none' || heartPath.getAttribute('fill') === null) {
				// Currently not saved, so save it
				type = 'save';
				
				// Add simple animation
				svg.classList.add('animate-favorite');
				setTimeout(() => {
					svg.classList.remove('animate-favorite');
				}, 400);
				
				// Show "Saved to favorites" popup next to this heart icon
				showSavedToFavoritesPopup(svg);
				
				heartPath.setAttribute('fill', '#e74c3c');
				heartPath.removeAttribute('stroke');
				svg.setAttribute('title', 'Click to remove from favorite');
				if (srOnlyElement) {
					srOnlyElement.textContent = 'Saved as favorited. Click to remove';
				}
				if (container) { container.classList.remove('not-saved'); container.classList.add('saved'); }
				if (stickyContainer) { stickyContainer.classList.remove('not-saved'); stickyContainer.classList.add('saved'); }
				btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
			} else {
				// Currently saved, so remove it
				type = 'remove';
				
				// Add simple animation
				svg.classList.add('animate-unfavorite');
				setTimeout(() => {
					svg.classList.remove('animate-unfavorite');
				}, 300);
				
				heartPath.setAttribute('fill', 'none');
				heartPath.setAttribute('stroke', '#747474');
				heartPath.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');
				svg.setAttribute('title', 'save to my favorites');
				if (srOnlyElement) {
					srOnlyElement.textContent = 'Click to save to my favorites';
				}
				if (container) { container.classList.remove('saved'); container.classList.add('not-saved'); }
				if (stickyContainer) { stickyContainer.classList.remove('saved'); stickyContainer.classList.add('not-saved'); }
				btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
			}
			
			// Make AJAX call
			let requestUrl = addUrl;
			if (type === 'remove') {
				requestUrl += '&remove=true';
			}
			
			document.body.style.cursor = 'wait';
			
			fetch(requestUrl)
				.then(response => response.text())
				.then(data => {
					const savedProgramsNum = document.getElementById('savedProgramsNum');
					if (savedProgramsNum) {
						savedProgramsNum.innerHTML = data;
					}
					document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
				})
				.catch(error => {
					console.error('Error:', error);
					document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
				});
			
			// GTM tracking (if dataLayer exists)
			if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
				// Check if this heart icon is from sticky navigation
				const isFromStickyNav = svg.id && svg.id.startsWith('sticky-save') || 
					(btn.id && btn.id.startsWith('sticky-div'));
				
				// Determine section based on source
				const section = isFromStickyNav ? "sticky navbar" : globalProgramName;
				
				dataLayer.push({
					event: 'select',
					action: type === 'save' ? 'click' : 'unclick',
					name: 'onclick',
					type: 'checkbox',
					region: 'main content',
					section: section,
					text: 'favorite',
					component: 'favorite icon'
				});
			}
			
			// Synchronize with sticky nav heart icon
			const isCurrentlySaved = (type === 'save');
			syncHeartIcons(acadPlan, isCurrentlySaved);
		});
	});
});

// Function to show "Saved to favorites" popup next to the clicked heart icon
function showSavedToFavoritesPopup(heartElement) {
	// Remove any existing popup
	const existingPopup = document.querySelector('.saved-to-favorites-popup');
	if (existingPopup) {
		existingPopup.remove();
	}
	
	// Create new popup element
	const popup = document.createElement('div');
	popup.className = 'saved-to-favorites-popup';
	popup.textContent = 'Saved to favorites';
	
	// Find the parent container to position relative to
	const parentContainer = heartElement.closest('.hero-buttons') || heartElement.closest('.sticky-nav-buttons') || heartElement.parentElement;
	
	if (parentContainer) {
		// Make parent container position relative if it isn't already
		const computedStyle = window.getComputedStyle(parentContainer);
		if (computedStyle.position === 'static') {
			parentContainer.style.position = 'relative';
		}
		
		// Add popup to the parent container
		parentContainer.appendChild(popup);
		
		// Position popup relative to the heart icon
		const heartRect = heartElement.getBoundingClientRect();
		const parentRect = parentContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
		
		// Calculate position relative to parent container
		const leftPosition = heartRect.left - parentRect.left + (heartRect.width / 2);
		
		popup.style.left = leftPosition + 'px';
		popup.style.top = (heartRect.bottom - parentRect.top + 10) + 'px';
		popup.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(10px)';
		
		// Show the popup with animation
		setTimeout(() => {
			popup.classList.add('show');
		}, 10);
		
		// Hide and remove the popup after 2 seconds
		setTimeout(() => {
			popup.classList.remove('show');
			setTimeout(() => {
				if (popup.parentElement) {
					popup.remove();
				}
			}, 300); // Wait for fade out animation
		}, 2000);
	}
}

// Function to synchronize heart icons between hero and sticky nav
function syncHeartIcons(acadPlan, isSaved) {
	// Find the other heart icon (if this was clicked from hero, sync sticky; if from sticky, sync hero)
	const heroHeart = document.getElementById('save' + acadPlan);
	const stickyHeart = document.getElementById('sticky-save' + acadPlan);
	const heroContainer = document.getElementById('div' + acadPlan);
	const stickyContainer = document.getElementById('sticky-div' + acadPlan);
	
	// Update both icons to match the current state
	[heroHeart, stickyHeart].forEach(function(heart) {
		if (heart) {
			const heartPath = heart.querySelector('path:last-child');
			// sr-only span is a child of the button (parent), not the SVG
			const parentBtn = heart.parentElement;
			const srOnlyElement = parentBtn ? parentBtn.querySelector('.sr-only') : null;
			
			if (heartPath) {
				if (isSaved) {
					heartPath.setAttribute('fill', '#e74c3c');
					heartPath.removeAttribute('stroke');
					heart.setAttribute('title', 'Click to remove from favorite');
					if (srOnlyElement) {
						srOnlyElement.textContent = 'Saved as favorited. Click to remove';
					}
				} else {
					heartPath.setAttribute('fill', 'none');
					heartPath.setAttribute('stroke', '#747474');
					heartPath.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');
					heart.setAttribute('title', 'save to my favorites');
					if (srOnlyElement) {
						srOnlyElement.textContent = 'Click to save to my favorites';
					}
				}
			}
			
			// Update aria-pressed on the button
			if (parentBtn && parentBtn.hasAttribute('aria-pressed')) {
				parentBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isSaved ? 'true' : 'false');
			}
		}
	});
	
	// Update container classes
	if (heroContainer) {
		heroContainer.classList.remove('saved', 'not-saved');
		heroContainer.classList.add(isSaved ? 'saved' : 'not-saved');
	}
	if (stickyContainer) {
		stickyContainer.classList.remove('saved', 'not-saved');
		stickyContainer.classList.add(isSaved ? 'saved' : 'not-saved');
	}
}

// Sticky Navigation Buttons Functionality
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const tabNav = document.querySelector('.tab-nav');
	const stickyButtons = document.querySelector('.sticky-nav-buttons');
	
	if (tabNav && stickyButtons) {
		// Create an intersection observer to detect when tab-nav becomes sticky
		const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
			function(entries) {
				entries.forEach(function(entry) {
					if (entry.isIntersecting) {
						// Tab nav is visible in its original position - hide buttons
						stickyButtons.style.display = 'none';
					} else {
						// Tab nav is sticky (not in original position) - show buttons
						stickyButtons.style.display = 'flex';
					}
				});
			},
			{
				root: null,
				rootMargin: '-115px 0px 0px 0px', // Account for the sticky top position
				threshold: 0
			}
		);
		
		// Observe the element right before the tab nav to detect when it goes out of view
		const heroSection = document.querySelector('.hero-section');
		if (heroSection) {
			observer.observe(heroSection);
		}
	}
});

// Nested accordion functionality for accelerated degrees
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	const accordionHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.accelerated-accordion .accordion-header');
	
	accordionHeaders.forEach(function(header) {
		const handleClick = function() {
			const targetId = this.getAttribute('data-target');
			const panel = document.getElementById(targetId);
			const isOpen = panel.classList.contains('show');
			
			// Close all panels in this accordion group
			const allHeaders = this.closest('.accelerated-accordion').querySelectorAll('.accordion-header');
			const allPanels = this.closest('.accelerated-accordion').querySelectorAll('.accordion-collapse');
			
			allHeaders.forEach(h => {
				h.classList.remove('active');
				h.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
				// Update aria-label for closed state
				const headerText = h.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
				h.setAttribute('aria-label', headerText + ', collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.');
			});
			
			allPanels.forEach(p => {
				if (p.classList.contains('show')) {
					p.style.height = p.scrollHeight + 'px';
					// Force reflow
					p.offsetHeight;
					p.classList.remove('show');
					p.classList.add('collapsing');
					setTimeout(() => {
						p.style.height = '0';
					}, 10);
					setTimeout(() => {
						p.classList.remove('collapsing');
						p.style.height = '';
					}, 360);
				}
			});
			
			// If this wasn't open, open it
			if (!isOpen && panel) {
				this.classList.add('active');
				this.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
				// Update aria-label for open state
				const headerText = this.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
				this.setAttribute('aria-label', headerText + ', expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.');
				
				panel.style.display = 'block';
				panel.classList.add('collapsing');
				panel.style.height = '0';
				// Force reflow
				panel.offsetHeight;
				setTimeout(() => {
					panel.style.height = panel.scrollHeight + 'px';
				}, 10);
				setTimeout(() => {
					panel.classList.remove('collapsing');
					panel.classList.add('show');
					// Keep explicit height to prevent jump
				}, 360);
			}
		};
		
		header.addEventListener('click', handleClick);
		
		// Add keyboard support for Enter and Space keys
		header.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
			if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
				e.preventDefault();
				handleClick.call(this);
			}
		});
		
		// Set initial aria-label
		const isExpanded = header.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
		const headerText = header.textContent.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
		header.setAttribute('aria-label', headerText + (isExpanded ? ', expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.' : ', collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.'));
	});
});

// Tooltip for tt_noTitle elements (math intensity, first required math course).
// Replaces the old jQuery clueTip plugin with a lightweight vanilla implementation.
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    // Create the tooltip container once
    var tooltip = document.createElement('div');
    tooltip.className = 'ds-tooltip';
    tooltip.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip');
    tooltip.style.display = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(tooltip);

    var triggers = document.querySelectorAll('.tt_noTitle');
    var hideTimer = null;

    function showTooltip(trigger) {
        var rel = trigger.getAttribute('rel');
        if (!rel) return;
        var source = document.querySelector(rel);
        if (!source) return;

        tooltip.textContent = source.textContent.trim();
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';

        // Position below the trigger, aligned to its left edge
        var rect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
        var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
        var scrollLeft = window.pageXOffset || document.documentElement.scrollLeft;
        var top = rect.bottom + scrollTop + 8;
        var left = rect.left + scrollLeft;

        // Keep it on screen
        if (left < 8) left = 8;
        if (left + tooltip.offsetWidth > document.documentElement.clientWidth - 8) {
            left = document.documentElement.clientWidth - tooltip.offsetWidth - 8;
        }

        tooltip.style.top = top + 'px';
        tooltip.style.left = left + 'px';

        // Fade in
        requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            tooltip.classList.add('is-visible');
        });
    }

    function hideTooltip() {
        tooltip.classList.remove('is-visible');
        // Hide after transition
        hideTimer = setTimeout(function () {
            tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        }, 200);
    }

    Array.prototype.forEach.call(triggers, function (trigger) {
        trigger.style.cursor = 'help';

        trigger.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
            showTooltip(trigger);
        });
        trigger.addEventListener('mouseleave', hideTooltip);
        trigger.addEventListener('focus', function () {
            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
            showTooltip(trigger);
        });
        trigger.addEventListener('blur', hideTooltip);
    });

    // Also hide on Escape
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && tooltip.classList.contains('is-visible')) {
            hideTooltip();
        }
    });
});

// Back to top button - show once scrolled away from the top
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    // Swap curriculum-hero image based on container height.
    // Uses ResizeObserver so it responds to dynamic content (accordion opens, window resize).
    // Image starts hidden and fades in once the correct source is determined.
    var heroContainer = document.querySelector('.curriculum-hero');
    var heroImg = heroContainer ? heroContainer.querySelector('.curriculum-hero-img') : null;
    var heroPicture = heroImg ? heroImg.closest('picture') : null;
    if (heroContainer && heroImg && heroImg.dataset.srcDesktop && heroImg.dataset.srcShort) {
        var updateHeroImage = function () {
            var h = heroContainer.offsetHeight;
            var useMobile = h < 500;
            var correctSrc = useMobile ? heroImg.dataset.srcShort : heroImg.dataset.srcDesktop;
            if (heroImg.getAttribute('src') !== correctSrc) {
                heroImg.setAttribute('src', correctSrc);
            }
            // Fade in once the correct image is set
            if (heroPicture && !heroPicture.classList.contains('is-visible')) {
                heroPicture.classList.add('is-visible');
            }
        };
        var ro = new ResizeObserver(updateHeroImage);
        ro.observe(heroContainer);
        updateHeroImage();
    }

    var backToTop = document.getElementById('back-to-top');
    if (!backToTop) return;

    function toggleBackToTop() {
        var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
        backToTop.classList.toggle('is-visible', scrollTop > 0);
    }

    backToTop.addEventListener('click', function () {
        // Icon-only control, so the text value is set explicitly rather than read from
        // the DOM. Follows the "copy plan code icon" naming precedent.
        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
            dataLayer.push({
                "event": "link",
                "action": "click",
                "name": "onclick",
                "type": "internal link",
                "region": "main content",
                "section": "back to top",
                "text": "back to top icon"
            });
        }

        // Respect users who prefer reduced motion by skipping the smooth scroll
        var prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: prefersReducedMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
    });

    window.addEventListener('scroll', toggleBackToTop);
    toggleBackToTop();
});

// bottomLink program term accordions.
// This page does not load ds.js, so the toggle is reimplemented here in vanilla JS.
// Native <button> handles Enter/Space, so no keypress shim is needed.
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    var headers = document.querySelectorAll('.section-bottom .programs_term');
    var status = document.getElementById('accordion-status');

    Array.prototype.forEach.call(headers, function (header) {
        header.addEventListener('click', function () {
            var content = header.parentNode.querySelector('.programs_term_content');
            var chevron = header.querySelector('.fa');
            var isOpening = header.classList.contains('ds_closed');

            header.classList.toggle('ds_closed', !isOpening);
            header.classList.toggle('ds_opened', isOpening);
            header.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpening ? 'true' : 'false');

            if (content) content.classList.toggle('no-display', !isOpening);

            // Swap the icon class directly rather than rewriting innerHTML like ds.js did
            if (chevron) {
                chevron.classList.toggle('fa-chevron-down', !isOpening);
                chevron.classList.toggle('fa-chevron-up', isOpening);
            }

            if (status) status.textContent = isOpening ? 'Expanded' : 'Collapsed';

            // This page uses dataLayer, not the legacy _gaq that ds.js referenced
            var heading = header.querySelector('h3');
            if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
                dataLayer.push({
                    "event": "collapse",
                    "action": isOpening ? "open" : "close",
                    "name": "onclick",
                    "type": "click",
                    "region": "main content",
                    "section": "program term definitions",
                    "text": heading ? heading.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() : ''
                });
            }
        });
    });
});

</script>

<script>
// Focus management for anchor links
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Add click handlers to all anchor links
    const anchorLinks = document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]');
    
    anchorLinks.forEach(function(link) {
        link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            const targetId = this.getAttribute('href').substring(1);
            const targetSection = document.getElementById(targetId);
            
            if (targetSection) {
                // Small delay to allow scroll to complete
                setTimeout(function() {
                    // Always focus the section heading (h2) rather than the first
                    // interactive element. This prevents scroll jumps into collapsed
                    // content and keeps the user at the top of the section.
                    var heading = targetSection.querySelector('h2');
                    if (heading) {
                        heading.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
                        heading.classList.add('no-focus-ring');
                        heading.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                    } else {
                        // Fallback: make the section itself focusable
                        targetSection.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
                        targetSection.classList.add('no-focus-ring');
                        targetSection.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                    }
                }, 100);
            }
        });
    });
});
</script>

															<strong>Global experience</strong>
															<p><p>As globalization continues to impact the way the world lives and works, international experiences have become vital to accountancy students. Through international study and internships, students can gain valuable skills that employers are looking for: communication and interpersonal skills, flexibility, motivation and a real-life perspective on business applications worldwide. Understanding international accounting methods helps students become more well-rounded and competitive in the accounting field. Accountancy students in particular benefit from thinking about participating in a <a href="https://goglobal.asu.edu/">Global Education program</a> early in their program. Students can leverage global opportunities and learning, which are great assets as they prepare for internships; these programs also provide distinct experiences that students can share with recruiters.</p><p>The W. P. Carey School of Business recommends <a href="https://goglobal.asu.edu/students/major/wp-carey/accountancy">these programs</a> for students majoring in accountancy.</p></p>

				<section id="rfi-section" aria-label="Request information">
					<div id="rfi-container"></div>
				</section>
				
				<script src="/resources/rfi/appRfi.standalone.umd.js?v=22"></script>
				<script>
					(function() {
						if (!window.AsuRfi || typeof window.AsuRfi.initRfi !== 'function') {
							console.error('AsuRfi is not loaded.');
							return;
						}
						window.AsuRfi.initRfi({
							targetSelector: '#rfi-container',
							props: {
								appPathFolder: '/resources/rfi',
								variant: 'rfiVariant3',
								programOfInterest: 'BAACCBS',
								
								studentType: 'undergrad',
								submissionUrl: '/t5/rfiSubmit',
								successRedirectUrl: '/bachelors/major/ASU00/BAACCBS/accountancy?rfiSuccess=true'
							}
						});

						// Show "Request info" buttons only when the RFI component actually renders its form.
						// The component briefly renders the form before checking rfiDisplay and removing it
						// for ineligible programs. We wait for the DOM to settle before revealing buttons.
						(function() {
							var container = document.getElementById('rfi-container');
							if (!container) return;

							function revealButtons() {
								var buttons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-rfi-button]');
								for (var i = 0; i < buttons.length; i++) {
									buttons[i].style.display = '';
								}
							}

							function formIsPresent() {
								return container.querySelector('.uds-rfi-form-wrapper') !== null;
							}

							var settleTimer = null;
							var observer = new MutationObserver(function() {
								clearTimeout(settleTimer);
								settleTimer = setTimeout(function() {
									if (formIsPresent()) {
										revealButtons();
									}
									observer.disconnect();
								}, 200);
							});
							observer.observe(container, { childList: true, subtree: true });

							setTimeout(function() {
								clearTimeout(settleTimer);
								observer.disconnect();
								if (formIsPresent()) {
									revealButtons();
								}
							}, 8000);
						})();
					})();
				</script>

  <a href="https://admission.asu.edu/wue">Web site</a>.

<div id="tt_save_auth" style="display: none">
  This program will be stored in your saved programs (upper right).<br />
</div>

<div id="tt_minor" style="display: none">
  A minor is an approved, coherent focus of academic study in a single
  discipline, other than the student's major, involving substantially fewer
  hours of credit than a corresponding major. Certain major and minor
  combinations may be deemed inappropriate either by the college or department
  of the major or minor.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathReq" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  This is only the first required math course. This program may contain
  additional math courses; See Curriculum for details.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathInt" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntGeneral" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the General level: MAT 142
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntModerate" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the Moderate level: MAT 117, MAT
  119, MAT 170, MAT 210, SOS 101, CPI 200
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntSubstantial" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the Substantial level: MAT 251,
  MAT 265. MAT 266, MAT 267, MAT 270, MAT 271, MAT 272, MAT 274, MAT 275
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent" style="display: none">
  Students pursuing concurrent degrees earn two distinct degrees and receive two
  diplomas. ASU offers students two ways to earn concurrent degrees: by choosing
  a predetermined combination or creating their own combination. Predetermined
  combinations have a single admissions application and one easy to follow major
  map. To add a concurrent degree to your existing degree, work with your
  academic advisor. Either way, concurrent degrees allow students to pursue
  their own personal or professional interests.
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent_undergrad" style="display: none">
  Concurrent degree programs are specially designed academic programs which
  provide high-achieving undergraduate students the opportunity to complete two
  distinct but complementary bachelor degrees at the same time. Students must
  meet minimum admissions standards for both programs and be accepted
  individually by both colleges offering the concurrent program.
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent_graduate" style="display: none">
  Concurrent degree programs are specially designed academic programs which
  provide high-achieving graduate students the opportunity to complete two
  distinct but complementary graduate degrees at the same time. Students must
  meet minimum admissions standards for both programs and be accepted
  individually by both colleges offering the concurrent program.
</div>

<div id="tt_accelerate_undergrad" style="display: none">
  Accelerated bachelor's plus master's degree programs are designed for
  high-achieving undergraduate students who want the opportunity to share
  undergraduate coursework with graduate coursework to accelerate completion of
  their master's degree. These programs feature the same high-quality curriculum
  taught by ASU's world-renowned faculty.
</div>

<div id="tt_accelerate_graduate" style="display: none">
  Accelerated bachelor's plus master's degree programs are designed for
  high-achieving undergraduate students who want the opportunity to share
  undergraduate coursework with graduate coursework to accelerate completion of
  their master's degree. These programs feature the same high-quality curriculum
  taught by ASU's world-renowned faculty.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Rolling" style="display: none">
  A rolling deadline means that applications will continue to be reviewed on a
  regular basis until the semester begins. International students should be
  mindful of visa deadlines to ensure there is time to produce necessary visa
  documents. Applicants are encouraged to complete and submit application
  materials as soon as possible for consideration.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Final" style="display: none">
  A final deadline means that all applications and application materials must be
  received by Graduate Admissions by the deadline date. Applications that are
  incomplete may not be considered after the final deadline. Applications that
  are submitted past the final deadline may not be considered.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Priority" style="display: none">
  A priority deadline means that applications submitted and completed before the
  priority deadline will receive priority consideration. Applications submitted
  after the priority deadlines will be reviewed in the order in which they were
  completed and on a space available basis. An application is complete after all
  materials are received by Graduate Admissions.
</div>

<div id="tt_stem_opt" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The 24-Month STEM OPT Extension is an additional benefit from United States
  Citizenship and Immigration Services that allows international students in F-1
  status who receive a bachelor’s, master’s, or doctoral degree in an approved
  STEM field to apply for a 24-month extension of their standard 12-month
  Post-OPT period, twice in a lifetime. This allows international students the
  opportunity to gain additional off-campus work experience in their field of
  study after completion of course studies and Post-OPT. The OPT extension does
  not apply to students completing a degree through ASU Online.
</div>

<section
  class="section section-bottom"
  style="font-size: 14px; background-color: #fafafa"
  aria-labelledby="program-terms-heading"

            <!-- <b>3 year programs</b>
					<br/><br/>

            <h4>Accelerated master's</h4>
            <p>
              These programs allow students to accelerate their studies to earn
              a bachelor's plus a master's degree in as few as five years (for
              some programs).
            </p>
            <p>
              Each program has requirements students must meet to be eligible
              for consideration. Students typically receive approval to pursue
              the accelerated master's during the junior year of their
              bachelor's degree program. Interested students can learn about
              eligibility requirements and
              <a href="https://acceleratedmasters.asu.edu/how-apply" onclick="trackLink('what are accelerated programs?', 'program term definitions')"
                >how to apply</a
              >.
            </p>

              than one college and provide opportunities for students to take
              advantage of the academic strengths of two academic units. Upon
              graduation, students are awarded one degree and one diploma
              conferred by two colleges.

<div id="tt_wue" style="display: none">
  Students from western states who select this major may be eligible for a
  reduced nonresident tuition rate of 150 percent of Arizona resident tuition
  plus all applicable fees. See more information and eligibility requirements on
  the Western Undergraduate Exchange (WUE) program
  <a href="https://admission.asu.edu/wue">Web site</a>.
</div>

<div id="tt_compare" style="display: none">
  check up to 3 programs to see a side-by-side comparison
</div>
<div id="tt_save_notauth" style="display: none">
  This program will be stored in your saved programs (upper right) only for this
  session. To save for later use, please <a href="https://weblogin.asu.edu/cgi-bin/login?callapp=https%3A%2F%2Fdegrees.asu.edu%2Fbachelors%3Finit%3Dfalse">sign in</a>.
</div>
<div id="tt_save_auth" style="display: none">
  This program will be stored in your saved programs (upper right).<br />
</div>

<div id="tt_minor" style="display: none">
  A minor is an approved, coherent focus of academic study in a single
  discipline, other than the student's major, involving substantially fewer
  hours of credit than a corresponding major. Certain major and minor
  combinations may be deemed inappropriate either by the college or department
  of the major or minor.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathReq" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  This is only the first required math course. This program may contain
  additional math courses; See Curriculum for details.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathInt" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required.
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntGeneral" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the General level: MAT 142
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntModerate" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the Moderate level: MAT 117, MAT
  119, MAT 170, MAT 210, SOS 101, CPI 200
</div>

<div id="tt_mathIntSubstantial" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The level of intensity represents a measure of the number and academic rigor
  of math courses required. Courses included in the Substantial level: MAT 251,
  MAT 265. MAT 266, MAT 267, MAT 270, MAT 271, MAT 272, MAT 274, MAT 275
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent" style="display: none">
  Students pursuing concurrent degrees earn two distinct degrees and receive two
  diplomas. ASU offers students two ways to earn concurrent degrees: by choosing
  a predetermined combination or creating their own combination. Predetermined
  combinations have a single admissions application and one easy to follow major
  map. To add a concurrent degree to your existing degree, work with your
  academic advisor. Either way, concurrent degrees allow students to pursue
  their own personal or professional interests.
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent_undergrad" style="display: none">
  Concurrent degree programs are specially designed academic programs which
  provide high-achieving undergraduate students the opportunity to complete two
  distinct but complementary bachelor degrees at the same time. Students must
  meet minimum admissions standards for both programs and be accepted
  individually by both colleges offering the concurrent program.
</div>

<div id="tt_concurrent_graduate" style="display: none">
  Concurrent degree programs are specially designed academic programs which
  provide high-achieving graduate students the opportunity to complete two
  distinct but complementary graduate degrees at the same time. Students must
  meet minimum admissions standards for both programs and be accepted
  individually by both colleges offering the concurrent program.
</div>

<div id="tt_accelerate_undergrad" style="display: none">
  Accelerated bachelor's plus master's degree programs are designed for
  high-achieving undergraduate students who want the opportunity to share
  undergraduate coursework with graduate coursework to accelerate completion of
  their master's degree. These programs feature the same high-quality curriculum
  taught by ASU's world-renowned faculty.
</div>

<div id="tt_accelerate_graduate" style="display: none">
  Accelerated bachelor's plus master's degree programs are designed for
  high-achieving undergraduate students who want the opportunity to share
  undergraduate coursework with graduate coursework to accelerate completion of
  their master's degree. These programs feature the same high-quality curriculum
  taught by ASU's world-renowned faculty.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Rolling" style="display: none">
  A rolling deadline means that applications will continue to be reviewed on a
  regular basis until the semester begins. International students should be
  mindful of visa deadlines to ensure there is time to produce necessary visa
  documents. Applicants are encouraged to complete and submit application
  materials as soon as possible for consideration.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Final" style="display: none">
  A final deadline means that all applications and application materials must be
  received by Graduate Admissions by the deadline date. Applications that are
  incomplete may not be considered after the final deadline. Applications that
  are submitted past the final deadline may not be considered.
</div>

<div id="tt_deadline_Priority" style="display: none">
  A priority deadline means that applications submitted and completed before the
  priority deadline will receive priority consideration. Applications submitted
  after the priority deadlines will be reviewed in the order in which they were
  completed and on a space available basis. An application is complete after all
  materials are received by Graduate Admissions.
</div>

<div id="tt_stem_opt" style="display: none" role="tooltip">
  The 24-Month STEM OPT Extension is an additional benefit from United States
  Citizenship and Immigration Services that allows international students in F-1
  status who receive a bachelor’s, master’s, or doctoral degree in an approved
  STEM field to apply for a 24-month extension of their standard 12-month
  Post-OPT period, twice in a lifetime. This allows international students the
  opportunity to gain additional off-campus work experience in their field of
  study after completion of course studies and Post-OPT. The OPT extension does
  not apply to students completing a degree through ASU Online.
</div>

    // Add ESC key functionality to close dropdown menus
    $(document).ready(function() {
      $(document).on('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 27) { // ESC key
          console.log('ESC pressed - closing ASU header dropdowns');
          // Close any open dropdowns in the ASU header navigation
          $('#asu-header-nav .navlist .navlink').removeClass('open');
          $('#asu-header-nav .dropdown-menu').hide();
          // Also try to close any Bootstrap dropdowns
          $('.dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.dropdown-menu').hide();
          // Try ASU components library specific classes
          $('.navlink.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.nav-dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
        }
      });
    });

                <h1>Academic Programs</h1>

                <div id="MainContent_divDP">Listed below are the Undergraduate Degrees offered by Michigan State University.     <div class="rvt-hero__inner">
      <div class="rvt-hero__body [ rvt-flow ]">
        <h1 class="rvt-hero__title">Chart your path to success in the College</h1>
        <div class="rvt-hero__teaser">
          <p>The <em>College of Arts and Sciences Bulletin</em> is a resource and roadmap that provides students with the information they need to make the most of their undergraduate education and to graduate from the College in a timely manner with a broad-based liberal arts degree. It is the official source of information regarding degree requirements, rules and regulations, programs of study, courses, and opportunities within and outside of individual schools, departments, and programs.</p>
        </div>
